/* ==========================================================
   Express App
========================================================== */

const app =
  express();

/* ==========================================================
   CORS Origins
========================================================== */

const configuredOrigins =
  process.env.CORS_ORIGINS
    ?.split(",")
    .map((origin) =>
      origin.trim()
    )
    .filter(Boolean) ?? [];

const allowedOrigins =
  new Set([
    "http://localhost:5173",

    "http://localhost:5174",

    ...configuredOrigins,
  ]);

/* ==========================================================
   CORS
========================================================== */

app.use(
  cors({
    origin:
      (
        requestOrigin,
        callback
      ) => {
        /*
          Allow requests without an Origin header
          such as Postman/server-to-server requests.
        */
        if (!requestOrigin) {
          callback(
            null,
            true
          );

          return;
        }

        if (
          allowedOrigins.has(
            requestOrigin
          )
        ) {
          callback(
            null,
            true
          );

          return;
        }

        callback(
          new Error(
            "CORS origin is not allowed."
          )
        );
      },

    credentials: true,

    methods: [
      "GET",
      "POST",
      "PUT",
      "PATCH",
      "DELETE",
      "OPTIONS",
    ],

    allowedHeaders: [
      "Content-Type",
      "Authorization",
    ],
  })
);

/* ==========================================================
   Body Parsers
========================================================== */

app.use(
  express.json({
    limit: "10mb",
  })
);

app.use(
  express.urlencoded({
    extended: true,

    limit: "10mb",
  })
);

/* ==========================================================
   Cookie Parser
========================================================== */

app.use(
  cookieParser()
);

/* ==========================================================
   Authentication

import mongoose from "mongoose";

export const connectDB = async () => {
  try {
    await mongoose.connect(
      process.env.MONGODB_URI as string
    );

    console.log(
      "âœ… MongoDB Connected"
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    process.exit(1);
  }
};

/**
 * Centralized AI usage/quota definitions.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * - Feature names are stable API-level identifiers.
 * - These identifiers are shared by:
 *   - AI orchestrator
 *   - AI quota service
 *   - Subscription usage model
 * - Do not duplicate these definitions elsewhere.
 */

export const AI_USAGE_FEATURES = [
  "resume",
  "ats",
  "interview",
  "coverLetter",
  "linkedin",
  "career",
  "recruiter",
  "general",
] as const;

export type AIUsageFeature =
  (typeof AI_USAGE_FEATURES)[number];

export const SUBSCRIPTION_PLANS = [
  "free",
  "trial",
  "pro-monthly",
  "pro-yearly",
] as const;

export type SubscriptionPlan =
  (typeof SUBSCRIPTION_PLANS)[number];

export interface AIPlanLimit {
  readonly daily: number;
}

/**
 * Centralized daily AI limits.
 *
 * V1 limits:
 *
 * free        -> 10 AI requests/day
 * trial       -> 25 AI requests/day
 * pro-monthly -> 100 AI requests/day
 * pro-yearly  -> 150 AI requests/day
 *
 * These values can later be moved to an admin/database
 * configuration layer without changing the orchestrator API.
 */
export const AI_PLAN_LIMITS = {
  free: {
    daily: 10,
  },

  trial: {
    daily: 25,
  },

  "pro-monthly": {
    daily: 100,
  },

  "pro-yearly": {
    daily: 150,
  },
} as const satisfies Record<
  SubscriptionPlan,
  AIPlanLimit
>;

/**
 * Maps logical AI features to the corresponding
 * Subscription.usage field.
 *
 * Example:
 *
 * "resume" -> subscription.usage.resumeToday
 */
export const AI_FEATURE_KEYS = {
  resume: "resumeToday",
  ats: "atsToday",
  interview: "interviewToday",
  coverLetter: "coverLetterToday",
  linkedin: "linkedinToday",
  career: "careerToday",
  recruiter: "recruiterToday",
  general: "generalToday",
} as const satisfies Record<
  AIUsageFeature,
  string
>;

export type AIUsageField =
  (typeof AI_FEATURE_KEYS)[AIUsageFeature];

import type {
  Request,
  Response,
} from "express";

import User from "../models/user.model.js";

import Subscription from "../models/subscription.model.js";

import generateToken from "../utils/generateToken.js";

import type {
  AuthRequest,
} from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

/* ==========================================================
   Types
========================================================== */

interface AuthResponseUser {
  id: string;

  name: string;

  email: string;

  isProUser: boolean;

  role: string;

  bio: string;

  title: string;

  location: string;

  phone: string;

  github: string;

  linkedin: string;

  portfolio: string;

  avatar: string;
}

/* ==========================================================
   Helpers
========================================================== */

function buildUserResponse(
  user: {
    _id: unknown;

    name: string;

    email: string;

    isProUser: boolean;

    role: string;

    bio?: string;

    title?: string;

    location?: string;

    phone?: string;

    github?: string;

    linkedin?: string;

    portfolio?: string;

    avatar?: string;
  }
): AuthResponseUser & {
  bio: string;
  title: string;
  location: string;
  phone: string;
  github: string;
  linkedin: string;
  portfolio: string;
  avatar: string;
} {
  return {
    id: String(user._id),

    name: user.name,

    email: user.email,

    isProUser:
      user.isProUser,

    role: user.role,

    bio: user.bio ?? "",

    title: user.title ?? "",

    location:
      user.location ?? "",

    phone:
      user.phone ?? "",

    github:
      user.github ?? "",

    linkedin:
      user.linkedin ?? "",

    portfolio:
      user.portfolio ?? "",

    avatar:
      user.avatar ?? "",
  };
}

function normalizeEmail(
  value: unknown
): string | null {
  if (
    typeof value !==
    "string"
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const email =
    value
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  if (
    !email ||
    email.length > 254
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const emailPattern =
    /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

  if (
    !emailPattern.test(
      email
    )
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return email;
}

/* ==========================================================
   REGISTER
========================================================== */

export const registerUser =
  async (
    req: Request,
    res: Response
  ): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      const {
        name,
        email,
        password,
      } = req.body ?? {};

      /* ====================================================
         Validate Name
      ==================================================== */

      if (
        typeof name !==
          "string" ||
        name.trim().length < 2 ||
        name.trim().length > 100
      ) {
        res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Name must be between 2 and 100 characters.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Validate Email
      ==================================================== */

      const normalizedEmail =
        normalizeEmail(
          email
        );

      if (!normalizedEmail) {
        res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Please provide a valid email address.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Validate Password
      ==================================================== */

      if (
        typeof password !==
          "string" ||
        password.length < 6 ||
        password.length > 128
      ) {
        res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Password must be between 6 and 128 characters.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Existing User
      ==================================================== */

      const existingUser =
        await User.exists({
          email:
            normalizedEmail,
        });

      if (existingUser) {
        res.status(409).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "An account with this email already exists.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Create User
      ==================================================== */

      const user =
        await User.create({
          name:
            name.trim(),

          email:
            normalizedEmail,

          password,

          role: "user",

          isProUser: false,
        });

      /* ====================================================
         Create Default Subscription
      ==================================================== */

      const subscription =
        await Subscription.create({
          user: user._id,
        });

      user.subscription =
        subscription._id;

      /* ====================================================
         Generate Tokens
      ==================================================== */

      const tokens =
        generateToken(
          res,
          user._id.toString()
        );

      user.refreshToken =
        tokens.refreshToken;

      await user.save();

      /* ====================================================
         Response
      ==================================================== */

      res.status(201).json({
        success: true,

        message:
          "Registration successful.",

        user:
          buildUserResponse(
            user
          ),

        token:
          tokens.accessToken,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Register error:",
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Unknown error"
      );

      res.status(500).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Registration failed.",
      });
    }
  };

/* ==========================================================
   LOGIN
========================================================== */

export const loginUser =
  async (
    req: Request,
    res: Response
  ): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      const {
        email,
        password,
      } = req.body ?? {};

      /* ====================================================
         Email
      ==================================================== */

      const normalizedEmail =
        normalizeEmail(
          email
        );

      if (
        !normalizedEmail
      ) {
        res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Please provide a valid email address.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Password
      ==================================================== */

      if (
        typeof password !==
          "string" ||
        password.length === 0 ||
        password.length > 128
      ) {
        res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Please provide a valid password.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Find User
      ==================================================== */

      const user =
        await User.findOne({
          email:
            normalizedEmail,
        }).select(
          "+password +refreshToken"
        );

      if (!user) {
        res.status(401).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Invalid email or password.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Password Verification
      ==================================================== */

      const passwordMatched =
        await user.comparePassword(
          password
        );

      if (
        !passwordMatched
      ) {
        res.status(401).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Invalid email or password.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Generate Tokens
      ==================================================== */

      const tokens =
        generateToken(
          res,
          user._id.toString()
        );

      /* ====================================================
         Rotate Refresh Token
      ==================================================== */

      user.refreshToken =
        tokens.refreshToken;

      await user.save();

      /* ====================================================
         Response
      ==================================================== */

      res.status(200).json({
        success: true,

        message:
          "Login successful.",

        user:
          buildUserResponse(
            user
          ),

        token:
          tokens.accessToken,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Login error:",
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Unknown error"
      );

      res.status(500).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Login failed.",
      });
    }
  };

/* ==========================================================
   LOGOUT
========================================================== */

export const logoutUser =
  async (
    req: Request,
    res: Response
  ): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      const refreshToken =
        req.cookies
          ?.refreshToken;

      if (
        typeof refreshToken ===
        "string" &&
        refreshToken.length > 0
      ) {
        await User.updateOne(
          {
            refreshToken,
          },

          {
            $unset: {
              refreshToken: 1,
            },
          }
        );
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Clear Access Cookie
      ==================================================== */

      res.clearCookie(
        "accessToken",
        {
          httpOnly: true,

          secure:
            process.env.NODE_ENV ===
            "production",

          sameSite:
            "strict",

          path: "/",
        }
      );

      /* ====================================================
         Clear Refresh Cookie
      ==================================================== */

      res.clearCookie(
        "refreshToken",
        {
          httpOnly: true,

          secure:
            process.env.NODE_ENV ===
            "production",

          sameSite:
            "strict",

          path: "/api/auth",
        }
      );

      res.status(200).json({
        success: true,

        message:
          "Logged out successfully.",
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Logout error:",
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Unknown error"
      );

      res.status(500).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Logout failed.",
      });
    }
  };

/* ==========================================================
   GET CURRENT USER
========================================================== */

export const getMe =
  async (
    req: AuthRequest,
    res: Response
  ): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      const userId =
        req.user?.id;

      if (
        typeof userId !==
          "string" ||
        !userId.trim()
      ) {
        res.status(401).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Authentication required.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Find Current User
      ==================================================== */

      const user =
        await User.findById(
          userId
        ).select(
          "-password -refreshToken"
        );

      if (!user) {
        res.status(404).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "User not found.",
        });

        return;
      }

      /* ====================================================
         Response
      ==================================================== */

      res.status(200).json({
        success: true,

        user:
          buildUserResponse(
            user
          ),
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Get current user error:",
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Unknown error"
      );

      res.status(500).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Unable to fetch user.",
      });
    }
  };

import type { Response } from "express";
import type { AuthRequest } from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

import {
  askAI,
} from "../lib/ai/ai-orchestrator.js";

export const getCareerInsights = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    const {
      overallScore,
      strengths = [],
      weaknesses = [],
    } = req.body;

    const prompt = `
You are a Senior FAANG Career Coach.

Interview Score:
${overallScore}

Strengths:
${strengths.join(", ")}

Weaknesses:
${weaknesses.join(", ")}

Return ONLY valid JSON.

{
  "focusAreas": [],
  "recommendedProjects": [],
  "careerAdvice": ""
}
`;

    if (!req.user) {
    return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Unauthorized",
    });
}

const result = await askAI({
    userId: req.user.id,
    prompt,
});

    const rawContent =
      result.content ?? "";

    const jsonMatch =
      rawContent.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);

    if (!jsonMatch) {
      throw new Error(
        "Invalid AI response"
      );
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      insights: JSON.parse(
        jsonMatch[0]
      ),
    });

  } catch (error) {

    console.error(
      "Career Insight Error:",
      error
    );

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message:
        "Failed to generate insights",
    });

  }
};

import type {
Request,
Response,
} from "express";

import {
generateCareerIntelligence,
} from "../services/ai/career-intelligence.service.js";

export async function careerIntelligenceController(
req: Request,
res: Response
) {
try {
const {
resumeText,
} = req.body;


if (
  typeof resumeText !== "string" ||
  resumeText.trim().length === 0
) {
  return res.status(400).json({
    success: false,
    message:
      "Resume text is required.",
  });
}

const userId =
  req.user?.id;

if (!userId) {
  return res.status(401).json({
    success: false,
    message:
      "Authentication required.",
  });
}

const cacheKey =
  `career-intelligence:${userId}`;

const profile =
  await generateCareerIntelligence(
    userId,
    cacheKey,
    resumeText
  );

return res.status(200).json({
  success: true,
  profile,
});

} catch (error) {
console.error(
"Career Intelligence Error:",
error
);

return res.status(500).json({
  success: false,
  message:
    "Failed to generate career intelligence.",
});
}
}

import type { Request, Response } from "express";
import InterviewSession from "../models/interview-session.model.js";

export const getDashboardStats =
async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {

    const interviews =
      await InterviewSession.find();

    const totalInterviews =
      interviews.length;

    const averageScore =
      totalInterviews > 0
        ? Math.round(
            interviews.reduce(
              (acc, item) =>
                acc +
                (item.overallScore || 0),
              0
            ) / totalInterviews
          )
        : 0;

    const strengthsMap:
      Record<string, number> = {};

    const weaknessesMap:
      Record<string, number> = {};

    interviews.forEach(
      (session) => {

        (session.strengths || [])
          .forEach((item: string) => {

            strengthsMap[item] =
              (strengthsMap[item] || 0)
              + 1;

          });

        (session.weaknesses || [])
          .forEach((item: string) => {

            weaknessesMap[item] =
              (weaknessesMap[item] || 0)
              + 1;

          });

      }
    );

    // const strongestArea =
    //   Object.keys(
    //     strengthsMap
    //   )[0] || "N/A";

      const strongestArea =
Object.entries(strengthsMap)
  .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0] ?? "N/A";

    // const weakestArea =
    //   Object.keys(
    //     weaknessesMap
    //   )[0] || "N/A";

      const weakestArea =
Object.entries(weaknessesMap)
  .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0] ?? "N/A";


      const hiringProbability =
  Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round(
      averageScore * 0.9
    )
  );

const recruiterReadiness =
  Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round(
      averageScore * 0.95
    )
  );

const profileCompletion =
  Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round(
      (
        totalInterviews / 10
      ) * 100
    )
  );

const atsAverage =
  Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round(
      averageScore * 0.92
    )
  );

    res.json({
  success: true,

  stats: {
    totalInterviews,

    averageScore,

    strongestArea,

    weakestArea,

    atsAverage,

    hiringProbability,

    recruiterReadiness,

    profileCompletion,
  },
});

  } catch (error) {

    console.error(error);

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
    });

  }
};


// NEW FUNCTION ADD

export const getResumeAnalytics = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    const interviews =
      await InterviewSession.find().sort({
        createdAt: 1,
      });

    const monthlyMap = new Map<
      string,
      number[]
    >();

    interviews.forEach((item) => {
      const month =
        new Date(item.createdAt).toLocaleString(
          "en-US",
          {
            month: "short",
          }
        );

      if (!monthlyMap.has(month)) {
        monthlyMap.set(month, []);
      }

      monthlyMap
        .get(month)!
        .push(item.overallScore || 0);
    });

    const analytics = Array.from(
      monthlyMap.entries()
    ).map(([month, scores]) => ({
      month,
      score:
        Math.round(
          scores.reduce(
            (a, b) => a + b,
            0
          ) / scores.length
        ),
    }));

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: analytics,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
    });
  }
};

import type {
  Response,
} from "express";

import type {
  AuthRequest,
} from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "../interview/history/interview-history.types.js";

import {
  generateInterviewQuestion,
} from "../services/ai/interview.service.js";

import {
  generateInterviewFeedback,
} from "../services/ai/interview-feedback.service.js";

// ============================================================
// Interview Types
// ============================================================

type InterviewType =
  | "skill"
  | "hr"
  | "mixed";

type InterviewDifficulty =
  | "beginner"
  | "intermediate"
  | "advanced";

type InterviewLanguage =
  | "english"
  | "hindi"
  | "hinglish"
  | "urdu";

// ============================================================
// Allowed Values
// ============================================================

const INTERVIEW_TYPES:
  readonly InterviewType[] = [
    "skill",
    "hr",
    "mixed",
  ];

const DIFFICULTIES:
  readonly InterviewDifficulty[] = [
    "beginner",
    "intermediate",
    "advanced",
  ];

const LANGUAGES:
  readonly InterviewLanguage[] = [
    "english",
    "hindi",
    "hinglish",
    "urdu",
  ];

// ============================================================
// Type Guards
// ============================================================

function isString(
  value: unknown
): value is string {
  return typeof value === "string";
}

function isStringArray(
  value: unknown
): value is string[] {
  return (
    Array.isArray(value) &&
    value.every(
      (item) =>
        typeof item === "string"
    )
  );
}

function isInterviewType(
  value: unknown
): value is InterviewType {
  return (
    isString(value) &&
    INTERVIEW_TYPES.includes(
      value as InterviewType
    )
  );
}

function isDifficulty(
  value: unknown
): value is InterviewDifficulty {
  return (
    isString(value) &&
    DIFFICULTIES.includes(
      value as InterviewDifficulty
    )
  );
}

function isLanguage(
  value: unknown
): value is InterviewLanguage {
  return (
    isString(value) &&
    LANGUAGES.includes(
      value as InterviewLanguage
    )
  );
}

// ============================================================
// History Validation
// ============================================================

function validateHistory(
  value: unknown
): value is InterviewHistoryItem[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  return value.every(
    (item) =>
      item &&
      typeof item === "object" &&
      typeof item.question ===
        "string" &&
      typeof item.skill ===
        "string" &&
      typeof item.competencyId ===
        "string" &&
      typeof item.competencyName ===
        "string" &&
      typeof item.difficulty ===
        "string" &&
      typeof item.createdAt ===
        "string"
  );
}

// ============================================================
// User ID
// ============================================================

function getUserId(
  req: AuthRequest
): string {
  const userId =
    req.user?.id;

  if (
    typeof userId !== "string" ||
    !userId.trim()
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Authenticated user ID is missing."
    );
  }

  return userId;
}

// ============================================================
// Generate Question
// ============================================================

export const getQuestion =
  async (
    req: AuthRequest,
    res: Response
  ) => {
    try {
      const userId =
        getUserId(req);

      const {
        profession: rawProfession,

        category,

        role,

        interviewType =
          "skill",

        skills,

        difficulty =
          "intermediate",

        language =
          "english",

        history = [],
      } = req.body ?? {};

      // ======================================================
      // Profession
      // ======================================================

      const profession =
        (
          isString(
            rawProfession
          ) &&
          rawProfession.trim()
        ) ||
        (
          isString(category) &&
          category.trim()
        ) ||
        "Software Engineer";

      // ======================================================
      // Interview Type
      // ======================================================

      if (
        !isInterviewType(
          interviewType
        )
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Invalid interview type.",
        });
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Skills
      // ======================================================

      let parsedSkills:
        string[] = [];

      if (
        isStringArray(skills) &&
        skills.length > 0
      ) {
        parsedSkills =
          skills
            .map(
              (skill) =>
                skill.trim()
            )
            .filter(Boolean);
      }

      if (
        parsedSkills.length === 0
      ) {
        parsedSkills = [
          profession,
        ];
      }

      if (
        parsedSkills.length > 30
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Too many skills provided.",
        });
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Difficulty
      // ======================================================

      if (
        !isDifficulty(
          difficulty
        )
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Invalid interview difficulty.",
        });
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Language
      // ======================================================

      if (
        !isLanguage(language)
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Invalid interview language.",
        });
      }

      // ======================================================
      // History
      // ======================================================

      if (
        !validateHistory(
          history
        )
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Invalid interview history.",
        });
      }

      if (
        history.length > 100
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Interview history is too large.",
        });
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Generate Question
      // ======================================================

      const result =
        await generateInterviewQuestion({
          userId,

          profession,

          role:
            isString(role)
              ? role.trim()
              : undefined,

          interviewType,

          skills:
            parsedSkills,

          difficulty,

          language,

          history,
        });

      return res.status(200).json({
        success: true,

        data: result,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Interview question generation error:",
        error
      );

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Failed to generate interview question.",
      });
    }
  };

// ============================================================
// Generate Feedback
// ============================================================

export const generateFeedback =
  async (
    req: AuthRequest,
    res: Response
  ) => {
    try {
      const userId =
        getUserId(req);

      const {
        questions,

        answers,

        skills,

        language =
          "english",

        history,
      } = req.body ?? {};

      // ======================================================
      // Questions
      // ======================================================

      let cleanedQuestions:
        string[] = [];

      if (
        isStringArray(
          questions
        )
      ) {
        cleanedQuestions =
          questions
            .map(
              (question) =>
                question.trim()
            )
            .filter(Boolean);
      } else if (
        Array.isArray(history)
      ) {
        cleanedQuestions =
          history
            .map(
              (item) =>
                typeof item?.question ===
                "string"
                  ? item.question.trim()
                  : ""
            )
            .filter(Boolean);
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Answers
      // ======================================================

      let cleanedAnswers:
        string[] = [];

      if (
        isStringArray(
          answers
        )
      ) {
        cleanedAnswers =
          answers.map(
            (answer) =>
              answer.trim()
          );
      } else if (
        Array.isArray(history)
      ) {
        cleanedAnswers =
          history
            .map(
              (item) =>
                typeof item?.answer ===
                "string"
                  ? item.answer.trim()
                  : ""
            )
            .filter(Boolean);
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Validate
      // ======================================================

      if (
        cleanedQuestions.length ===
          0 ||
        cleanedAnswers.length ===
          0
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Questions and answers are required.",
        });
      }

      if (
        cleanedQuestions.length !==
        cleanedAnswers.length
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Questions and answers must contain the same number of items.",
        });
      }

      if (
        cleanedQuestions.length >
        50
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Too many interview answers submitted.",
        });
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Skills
      // ======================================================

      let cleanedSkills:
        string[] = [];

      if (
        isStringArray(skills) &&
        skills.length > 0
      ) {
        cleanedSkills =
          skills
            .map(
              (skill) =>
                skill.trim()
            )
            .filter(Boolean);
      } else if (
        Array.isArray(history)
      ) {
        cleanedSkills =
          Array.from(
            new Set(
              history
                .map(
                  (item) =>
                    typeof item?.skill ===
                    "string"
                      ? item.skill.trim()
                      : ""
                )
                .filter(Boolean)
            )
          );
      }

      if (
        cleanedSkills.length ===
        0
      ) {
        cleanedSkills = [
          "General",
        ];
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Language
      // ======================================================

      if (
        !isLanguage(language)
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,

          message:
            "Invalid interview language.",
        });
      }

      // ======================================================
      // Generate Feedback
      // ======================================================

      const result =
        await generateInterviewFeedback({
          userId,

          questions:
            cleanedQuestions,

          answers:
            cleanedAnswers,

          skills:
            cleanedSkills,

          language,
        });

      return res.status(200).json({
        success: true,

        data: result,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Interview feedback generation error:",
        error
      );

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Interview feedback generation failed.",
      });
    }
  };

import type { Response } from "express";

export const saveInterview =
async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {

    const session =
      await InterviewSession.create({
        user: req.user?.id,

        ...req.body,
      });

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      session,
    });

  } catch (error) {

    console.error(error);

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
    });

  }

async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {

      await InterviewSession.find({
        user: req.user?.id,
      }).sort({
        createdAt: -1,
      });

    res.json({
      success: true,
      sessions,
    });

  } catch (error) {

    console.error(error);

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
    });

  }
};

import type { Response } from "express";

import type {
  AuthRequest,
} from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "../interview/history/interview-history.types.js";

import {
  generateInterviewQuestion,
} from "../services/ai/interview.service.js";

import {
  generateInterviewFeedback,
} from "../services/ai/interview-feedback.service.js";

import {
  isInterviewType,
  isInterviewDifficulty,
  isInterviewLanguage,
} from "../interview/types/interview.types.js";

import type {
  InterviewType,
  InterviewDifficulty,
  InterviewLanguage,
} from "../interview/types/interview.types.js";

/* =========================================================
   Runtime Limits
========================================================= */

const MAX_SKILLS = 30;

const MAX_HISTORY_ITEMS = 100;

const MAX_FEEDBACK_QUESTIONS = 50;

const MAX_STRING_LENGTH = 500;

/* =========================================================
   Utility Type Guards
========================================================= */

function isString(
  value: unknown
): value is string {
  return typeof value === "string";
}

function isStringArray(
  value: unknown
): value is string[] {
  return (
    Array.isArray(value) &&
    value.every(
      (item) =>
        typeof item === "string"
    )
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   Safe String
========================================================= */

function cleanString(
  value: unknown
): string {
  if (!isString(value)) {
    return "";
  }

  return value
    .trim()
    .slice(0, MAX_STRING_LENGTH);
}

/* =========================================================
   Clean String Array
========================================================= */

function cleanStringArray(
  value: unknown
): string[] {
  if (!isStringArray(value)) {
    return [];
  }

  return [
    ...new Set(
      value
        .map((item) =>
          item.trim()
        )
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((item) =>
          item.slice(
            0,
            MAX_STRING_LENGTH
          )
        )
    ),
  ];
}

/* =========================================================
   History Validation
========================================================= */

function validateHistory(
  value: unknown
): value is InterviewHistoryItem[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    value.length >
    MAX_HISTORY_ITEMS
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  return value.every(
    (item) => {
      if (
        !item ||
        typeof item !== "object"
      ) {
        return false;
      }

      const historyItem =
        item as Partial<InterviewHistoryItem>;

      return (
        typeof historyItem.question ===
          "string" &&
        typeof historyItem.skill ===
          "string" &&
        typeof historyItem.competencyId ===
          "string" &&
        typeof historyItem.competencyName ===
          "string" &&
        typeof historyItem.difficulty ===
          "string" &&
        isInterviewDifficulty(
          historyItem.difficulty
        ) &&
        typeof historyItem.createdAt ===
          "string"
      );
    }
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   User ID
========================================================= */

function getUserId(
  req: AuthRequest
): string {
  const userId =
    req.user?.id;

  if (
    typeof userId !== "string" ||
    !userId.trim()
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Authenticated user ID is missing."
    );
  }

  return userId.trim();
}

/* =========================================================
   Generate Interview Question
========================================================= */

export const getQuestion = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    const userId =
      getUserId(req);

    const body =
      req.body ?? {};

    const rawProfession =
      body.profession;

    const category =
      body.category;

    const role =
      body.role;

    const rawInterviewType =
      body.interviewType ??
      "skill";

    const skills =
      body.skills;

    const rawDifficulty =
      body.difficulty ??
      "intermediate";

    const rawLanguage =
      body.language ??
      "english";

    const history =
      body.history ?? [];

    /* =====================================================
       Profession
    ===================================================== */

    const profession =
      cleanString(
        rawProfession
      ) ||
      cleanString(
        category
      ) ||
      "Software Engineer";

    /* =====================================================
       Role
    ===================================================== */

    const cleanedRole =
      cleanString(role);

    /* =====================================================
       Interview Type
    ===================================================== */

    if (
      !isInterviewType(
        rawInterviewType
      )
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Invalid interview type.",
        code:
          "INVALID_INTERVIEW_TYPE",
      });
    }

    const interviewType:
      InterviewType =
        rawInterviewType;

    /* =====================================================
       Skills
    ===================================================== */

    let parsedSkills =
      cleanStringArray(
        skills
      );

    if (
      parsedSkills.length === 0
    ) {
      parsedSkills = [
        profession,
      ];
    }

    if (
      parsedSkills.length >
      MAX_SKILLS
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Too many skills provided.",
        code:
          "TOO_MANY_SKILLS",
      });
    }

    /* =====================================================
       Difficulty
    ===================================================== */

    if (
      !isInterviewDifficulty(
        rawDifficulty
      )
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Invalid interview difficulty.",
        code:
          "INVALID_INTERVIEW_DIFFICULTY",
      });
    }

    const difficulty:
      InterviewDifficulty =
        rawDifficulty;

    /* =====================================================
       Language
    ===================================================== */

    if (
      !isInterviewLanguage(
        rawLanguage
      )
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Invalid interview language.",
        code:
          "INVALID_INTERVIEW_LANGUAGE",
      });
    }

    const language:
      InterviewLanguage =
        rawLanguage;

    /* =====================================================
       History
    ===================================================== */

    if (
      !validateHistory(
        history
      )
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Invalid interview history.",
        code:
          "INVALID_INTERVIEW_HISTORY",
      });
    }

    /* =====================================================
       AI Question Generation
    ===================================================== */

    const result =
      await generateInterviewQuestion({
        userId,

        profession,

        role:
          cleanedRole ||
          undefined,

        interviewType,

        skills:
          parsedSkills,

        difficulty,

        language,

        history,
      });

    /* =====================================================
       Success
    ===================================================== */

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,

      data: result,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Interview question generation error:",
      error
    );

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,

      message:
        "Failed to generate interview question.",

      code:
        "INTERVIEW_QUESTION_GENERATION_FAILED",
    });
  }
};

/* =========================================================
   Generate Interview Feedback
========================================================= */

export const generateFeedback =
  async (
    req: AuthRequest,
    res: Response
  ) => {
    try {
      const userId =
        getUserId(req);

      const body =
        req.body ?? {};

      const questions =
        body.questions;

      const answers =
        body.answers;

      const skills =
        body.skills;

      const history =
        body.history;

      const rawLanguage =
        body.language ??
        "english";

      /* =====================================================
         Language
      ===================================================== */

      if (
        !isInterviewLanguage(
          rawLanguage
        )
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message:
            "Invalid interview language.",
          code:
            "INVALID_INTERVIEW_LANGUAGE",
        });
      }

      const language:
        InterviewLanguage =
        rawLanguage;

      /* =====================================================
         Questions
      ===================================================== */

      let cleanedQuestions: string[] =
        [];

      if (
        isStringArray(
          questions
        )
      ) {
        cleanedQuestions =
          questions
            .map((question) =>
              question.trim()
            )
            .filter(Boolean);
      } else if (
        Array.isArray(history)
      ) {
        cleanedQuestions =
          history
            .map((item) =>
              item &&
              typeof item ===
                "object" &&
              typeof item.question ===
                "string"
                ? item.question.trim()
                : ""
            )
            .filter(Boolean);
      }

      /* =====================================================
         Answers
      ===================================================== */

      let cleanedAnswers: string[] =
        [];

      if (
        isStringArray(
          answers
        )
      ) {
        cleanedAnswers =
          answers.map((answer) =>
            answer.trim()
          );
      } else if (
        Array.isArray(history)
      ) {
        cleanedAnswers =
          history
            .map((item) =>
              item &&
              typeof item ===
                "object" &&
              typeof item.answer ===
                "string"
                ? item.answer.trim()
                : ""
            )
            .filter(Boolean);
      }

      /* =====================================================
         Required Data
      ===================================================== */

      if (
        cleanedQuestions.length ===
          0 ||
        cleanedAnswers.length ===
          0
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message:
            "Questions and answers are required.",
          code:
            "QUESTIONS_AND_ANSWERS_REQUIRED",
        });
      }

      /* =====================================================
         Array Length
      ===================================================== */

      if (
        cleanedQuestions.length !==
        cleanedAnswers.length
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message:
            "Questions and answers must contain the same number of items.",
          code:
            "QUESTION_ANSWER_LENGTH_MISMATCH",
        });
      }

      /* =====================================================
         Feedback Limit
      ===================================================== */

      if (
        cleanedQuestions.length >
        MAX_FEEDBACK_QUESTIONS
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message:
            "Too many interview answers submitted.",
          code:
            "TOO_MANY_INTERVIEW_ANSWERS",
        });
      }

      /* =====================================================
         Skills
      ===================================================== */

      let cleanedSkills =
        cleanStringArray(
          skills
        );

      if (
        cleanedSkills.length === 0 &&
        Array.isArray(history)
      ) {
        cleanedSkills =
          Array.from(
            new Set(
              history
                .map((item) =>
                  item &&
                  typeof item ===
                    "object" &&
                  typeof item.skill ===
                    "string"
                    ? item.skill.trim()
                    : ""
                )
                .filter(Boolean)
            )
          );
      }

      if (
        cleanedSkills.length === 0
      ) {
        cleanedSkills = [
          "General",
        ];
      }

      if (
        cleanedSkills.length >
        MAX_SKILLS
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message:
            "Too many skills provided.",
          code:
            "TOO_MANY_SKILLS",
        });
      }

      /* =====================================================
         AI Feedback Generation
      ===================================================== */

      const result =
        await generateInterviewFeedback({
          userId,

          questions:
            cleanedQuestions,

          answers:
            cleanedAnswers,

          skills:
            cleanedSkills,

          language,
        });

      /* =====================================================
         Success
      ===================================================== */

      return res.status(200).json({
        success: true,

        data: result,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Interview feedback generation error:",
        error
      );

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Interview feedback generation failed.",

        code:
          "INTERVIEW_FEEDBACK_GENERATION_FAILED",
      });
    }
  };

import type { Response } from "express";
import { randomUUID } from "crypto";

import JobApplication, {
  type IJobApplication,
  type JobStatus,
  JOB_STATUSES,
} from "../models/JobApplication.model.js";

import type {
  AuthRequest,
} from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

/* ==========================================================
   Serialize
========================================================== */

function toJobItem(job: IJobApplication) {
  return {
    id: job._id.toString(),
    company: job.company,
    position: job.position,
    location: job.location,
    employmentType: job.employmentType,
    workMode: job.workMode,
    priority: job.priority,
    status: job.status,
    salary: job.salary,
    appliedDate: job.appliedDate,
    deadline: job.deadline,
    jobUrl: job.jobUrl,
    description: job.description,
    notes: job.notes,
    interview: job.interview,
    timeline: job.timeline ?? [],
    createdAt: job.createdAt.toISOString(),
    updatedAt: job.updatedAt.toISOString(),
  };
}

function isJobStatus(
  value: unknown
): value is JobStatus {
  return (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    (JOB_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(
      value
    )
  );
}

/* ==========================================================
   Create Job
========================================================== */

export const createJob = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const {
      company,
      position,
      location,
      employmentType,
      workMode,
      priority,
      status,
      salary,
      deadline,
      jobUrl,
      description,
      notes,
    } = req.body;

    if (
      !company?.trim() ||
      !position?.trim() ||
      !location?.trim()
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Company, position, and location are required.",
      });
    }

    const initialStatus: JobStatus =
      isJobStatus(status) ? status : "wishlist";

    const job = await JobApplication.create({
      user: req.user.id,
      company: company.trim(),
      position: position.trim(),
      location: location.trim(),
      employmentType:
        employmentType ?? "full-time",
      workMode: workMode ?? "remote",
      priority: priority ?? "medium",
      status: initialStatus,
      salary,
      appliedDate: new Date().toISOString(),
      deadline,
      jobUrl,
      description,
      notes,
      timeline: [
        {
          id: randomUUID(),
          title: "Job created",
          description:
            "Application added to tracker.",
          status: initialStatus,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
      ],
    });

    return res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Job created successfully.",
      data: toJobItem(job),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to create job.",
    });
  }
};

/* ==========================================================
   Get All Jobs
========================================================== */

export const getJobs = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const jobs = await JobApplication.find({
      user: req.user.id,
    }).sort({ createdAt: -1 });

    const data = jobs.map(toJobItem);

    return res.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Jobs fetched successfully.",
      data,
      // Backward-compatible alias for dashboard widget
      jobs: data,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to fetch jobs.",
    });
  }
};

/* ==========================================================
   Get Dashboard Stats
========================================================== */

export const getJobStats = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const jobs = await JobApplication.find({
      user: req.user.id,
    }).select("status");

    const stats = {
      total: jobs.length,
      applied: 0,
      interviews: 0,
      offers: 0,
      accepted: 0,
      rejected: 0,
      wishlist: 0,
    };

    for (const job of jobs) {
      switch (job.status) {
        case "wishlist":
          stats.wishlist += 1;
          break;
        case "applied":
        case "screening":
          stats.applied += 1;
          break;
        case "interview":
        case "technical":
        case "hr":
          stats.interviews += 1;
          break;
        case "offer":
          stats.offers += 1;
          break;
        case "accepted":
          stats.accepted += 1;
          break;
        case "rejected":
          stats.rejected += 1;
          break;
      }
    }

    return res.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Job stats fetched successfully.",
      data: stats,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to fetch job stats.",
    });
  }
};

      message: "Unable to fetch job.",
    });
  }

/* ==========================================================
   Update Job
========================================================== */

export const updateJob = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const allowedFields = [
      "company",
      "position",
      "location",
      "employmentType",
      "workMode",
      "priority",
      "status",
      "salary",
      "deadline",
      "jobUrl",
      "description",
      "notes",
      "interview",
    ] as const;

    const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {};

    for (const field of allowedFields) {
      if (req.body[field] !== undefined) {
        updates[field] = req.body[field];
      }
    }

    const job =
      await JobApplication.findOneAndUpdate(
        {
          _id: req.params.id,
          user: req.user.id,
        },
        updates,
        { new: true }
      );

    if (!job) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Job not found.",
      });
    }

    return res.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Job updated successfully.",
      data: toJobItem(job),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to update job.",
    });
  }
};

   Delete Job

export const updateJobStatus = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const { status } = req.body;

    if (!isJobStatus(status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid job status.",
      });
    }

    const job = await JobApplication.findOne({
      _id: req.params.id,
      user: req.user.id,
    });

    if (!job) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Job not found.",
      });
    }

    job.status = status;
    job.timeline.push({
      id: randomUUID(),
      title: "Status updated",
      description: `Status changed to ${status}.`,
      status,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });

    await job.save();

    return res.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Status updated successfully.",
      data: toJobItem(job),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to update status.",
    });
  }
};

export const scheduleInterview = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const { interview } = req.body;

    if (
      !interview?.date ||
      !interview?.time
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Interview date and time are required.",
      });
    }

    const job = await JobApplication.findOne({
      _id: req.params.id,
      user: req.user.id,
    });

    if (!job) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Job not found.",
      });
    }

    job.interview = interview;
    job.status = "interview";
    job.timeline.push({
      id: randomUUID(),
      title: "Interview scheduled",
      description: `Interview on ${interview.date} at ${interview.time}.`,
      status: "interview",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });

    await job.save();

    return res.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Interview scheduled successfully.",
      data: toJobItem(job),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to schedule interview.",
    });
  }
};

/* ==========================================================
   Add Timeline Event
========================================================== */

export const addTimelineEvent = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const { timelineItem } = req.body;

    if (
      !timelineItem?.title ||
      !isJobStatus(timelineItem.status)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Timeline title and valid status are required.",
      });
    }

    const job = await JobApplication.findOne({
      _id: req.params.id,
      user: req.user.id,
    });

    if (!job) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Job not found.",
      });
    }

    job.timeline.push({
      id: timelineItem.id || randomUUID(),
      title: timelineItem.title,
      description:
        timelineItem.description ?? "",
      status: timelineItem.status,
      createdAt:
        timelineItem.createdAt ||
        new Date().toISOString(),
    });

    await job.save();

    return res.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Timeline event added.",
      data: toJobItem(job),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to add timeline event.",
    });
  }
};

import type { Response } from "express";

import type {
  AuthRequest,
} from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

import {
  optimizeLinkedInProfile,
  getLinkedInHistory,
  deleteLinkedInHistory,
} from "../services/linkedin/linkedin-optimizer.service.js";

import type {
  LinkedInOptimizeRequest,
} from "../types/linkedin/linkedin.types.js";

/* ==========================================================
   LinkedIn Optimizer Controller
========================================================== */

export async function optimizeLinkedInController(
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const body =
      req.body as LinkedInOptimizeRequest;

    const profile = body.profile;

    if (!profile) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Profile payload is required.",
      });
    }

    if (!profile.headline?.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Headline is required.",
      });
    }

    if (!profile.summary?.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Summary is required.",
      });
    }

    if (!profile.targetRole?.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Target role is required.",
      });
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(profile.skills)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Skills must be an array.",
      });
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(profile.achievements)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Achievements must be an array.",
      });
    }

    const result = await optimizeLinkedInProfile(
      {
        profile: {
          fullName: profile.fullName?.trim() || "",
          headline: profile.headline.trim(),
          currentRole:
            profile.currentRole?.trim() || "",
          yearsOfExperience: Number(
            profile.yearsOfExperience ?? 0
          ),
          location: profile.location?.trim() || "",
          skills: profile.skills,
          summary: profile.summary.trim(),
          achievements: profile.achievements,
          targetRole: profile.targetRole.trim(),
          industry: profile.industry?.trim() || "",
        },
        tone: body.tone,
        language: body.language,
      },
      req.user.id
    );

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message:
        "LinkedIn profile optimized successfully.",
      data: result,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "LinkedIn Optimizer Error:",
      error
    );

    const message =
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unable to optimize LinkedIn profile.";

    const status =
      message.toLowerCase().includes("quota") ||
      message.toLowerCase().includes("limit")
        ? 429
        : 500;

    return res.status(status).json({
      success: false,
      message,
    });
  }
}

export async function getLinkedInHistoryController(
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const history = await getLinkedInHistory(
      req.user.id
    );

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "History fetched successfully.",
      data: history,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to load LinkedIn history.",
    });
  }
}

export async function deleteLinkedInHistoryController(
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const id = Array.isArray(req.params.id)
      ? req.params.id[0]
      : req.params.id;

    if (!id) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "History id is required.",
      });
    }

    const deleted = await deleteLinkedInHistory(
      req.user.id,
      id
    );

    if (!deleted) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "History item not found.",
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "History deleted successfully.",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message:
        "Unable to delete LinkedIn history.",
    });
  }
}

  return res.status(501).json({

import type { Request, Response } from "express";

import {
runRecruiterSimulation,
} from "../services/ai/recruiter-simulation.service.js";

export const recruiterSimulationController = async (
req: Request,
res: Response
) => {
try {
const {
resumeAnalysis,
interviewAnalysis,
careerProfile,
} = req.body;


if (
  !resumeAnalysis ||
  !interviewAnalysis ||
  !careerProfile
) {
  return res.status(400).json({
    success: false,
    message:
      "Missing required recruiter simulation data.",
  });
}

const userId = req.user?.id;

if (!userId) {
  return res.status(401).json({
    success: false,
    message: "Authentication required.",
  });
}

const simulation =
  await runRecruiterSimulation(
    userId,
    {
      resumeAnalysis,
      interviewAnalysis,
      careerProfile,
    }
  );

return res.status(200).json({
  success: true,
  simulation,
});


} catch (error) {
console.error(
"Recruiter Simulation Error:",
error
);

return res.status(500).json({
  success: false,
  message:
    "Recruiter simulation failed.",
});


}
};

import type { Response } from "express";
import type { AuthRequest } from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

import {
  buildResume,
} from "../services/ai/resume-builder/services/resume-builder-ai.service.js";

export async function generateResumeController(
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) {
  try {
    const {
      resumeText,
      language = "english",
    } = req.body;

    if (
      typeof resumeText !== "string" ||
      !resumeText.trim()
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "resumeText is required.",
      });
    }

    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const result =
      await buildResume(
        req.user.id,
        resumeText,
        language
      );

    return res.json({
      success: true,
      data: result,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Resume Builder error:",
      error
    );

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Resume Builder failed.",
    });
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\controllers\resume-upload.controller.ts

import type {
  Request,
  Response,
} from "express";

import fs from "node:fs/promises";

import {
  extractResumeText,
} from "../services/resume-parser.service.js";

export const uploadResume =
  async (
    req: Request,
    res: Response
  ) => {
    const uploadedFile =
      req.file;

    try {
      if (!uploadedFile) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message:
            "Resume file is required.",
        });
      }

      const resumeText =
        await extractResumeText(
          uploadedFile.path
        );

      if (
        !resumeText ||
        !resumeText.trim()
      ) {
        return res.status(422).json({
          success: false,
          message:
            "Unable to extract readable text from the uploaded resume.",
        });
      }

      return res.status(200).json({
        success: true,
        message:
          "Resume uploaded and parsed successfully.",
        data: {
          resumeText:
            resumeText.trim(),
          file: {
            originalName:
              uploadedFile.originalname,
            mimeType:
              uploadedFile.mimetype,
            size:
              uploadedFile.size,
          },
        },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Resume Upload Error:",
        error
      );

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Resume upload or parsing failed.",
      });
    } finally {
      if (uploadedFile?.path) {
        try {
          await fs.unlink(
            uploadedFile.path
          );
        } catch (cleanupError) {
          console.error(
            "Resume temporary file cleanup failed:",
            cleanupError
          );
        }
      }
    }
  };

import type { Request, Response } from "express";

import Settings from "../models/settings.model.js";

import type { AuthRequest } from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

/* ==========================================================\
   Get My Settings
========================================================== */

export const getMySettings = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    let settings = await Settings.findOne({
      user: req.user.id,
    });

    if (!settings) {
      settings = await Settings.create({
        user: req.user.id,
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      settings,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to fetch settings.",
    });
  }
};

/* ==========================================================\
   Update My Settings
========================================================== */

export const updateMySettings = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    if (!req.user?.id) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required.",
      });
    }

    const {
      darkMode,
      sendEmailNotifications,
      sendSMSNotifications,
      profileVisibility,
    } = req.body;

    const settings = await Settings.findOneAndUpdate(
      {
        user: req.user.id,
      },
      {
        darkMode,
        sendEmailNotifications,
        sendSMSNotifications,
        profileVisibility,
      },
      {
        new: true,
      }
    );

    if (!settings) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Settings not found.",
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Settings updated successfully.",
      settings,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to update settings.",
    });
  }
};

export const upgradePlan = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    const user = (req as any).user;

    const {
      plan,
      expiresAt,
      razorpayOrderId,
      razorpayPaymentId,
      razorpaySignature,
      paymentMethod,
      amountPaid,
      currency,
    } = req.body;

    const subscription =
      await Subscription.findOne({
        user: user.id,
      });

    if (!subscription) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Subscription not found",
      });
    }

    subscription.plan = plan;

    subscription.status = "active";

    subscription.expiresAt =
      expiresAt;

    subscription.razorpayOrderId =
      razorpayOrderId;

    subscription.razorpayPaymentId =
      razorpayPaymentId;

    subscription.razorpaySignature =
      razorpaySignature;

    subscription.paymentMethod =
      paymentMethod;

    subscription.amountPaid =
      amountPaid;

    subscription.currency =
      currency;

    await subscription.save();

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      subscription,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message:
        "Unable to upgrade plan",
    });
  }
};
