import type { Request, Response } from "express";
import Subscription from "../models/subscription.model.js";

export const getUsage = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
) => {
  try {
    const user = (req as any).user;

    let subscription =
      await Subscription.findOne({
        user: user.id,
      });

    if (!subscription) {
      subscription =
        await Subscription.create({
          user: user.id,

          plan: "trial",

          status: "active",
        });
    }

    const plan = subscription.plan;

    const limits =
      plan === "free"
        ? {
            resume: 5,
            ats: 5,
            interview: 20,
            coverLetter: 5,
          }
        : null;

    return res.json({
      success: true,

      plan,

      status: subscription.status,

      usage: subscription.usage,

      limits,

      trialEnd: subscription.trialEnd,

      expiresAt: subscription.expiresAt,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to fetch usage",
    });
  }
};


============================================================

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\controllers\user.controller.ts

============================================================

import type {
  Request,
  Response,
} from "express";

import User from "../models/user.model.js";

import type {
  AuthRequest,
} from "../middleware/auth.middleware.js";

/* ==========================================================
   Types
========================================================== */

interface ProfileUpdateBody {
  name?: unknown;

  bio?: unknown;

  title?: unknown;

  location?: unknown;

  phone?: unknown;

  github?: unknown;

  linkedin?: unknown;

  portfolio?: unknown;

  avatar?: unknown;
}

/* ==========================================================
   Allowed Profile Fields
========================================================== */

const PROFILE_FIELDS = [
  "name",
  "bio",
  "title",
  "location",
  "phone",
  "github",
  "linkedin",
  "portfolio",
  "avatar",
] as const;

type ProfileField =
  (typeof PROFILE_FIELDS)[number];

/* ==========================================================
   Limits
========================================================== */

const FIELD_LIMITS: Record<
  ProfileField,
  number
> = {
  name: 100,

  bio: 500,

  title: 100,

  location: 100,

  phone: 30,

  github: 200,

  linkedin: 200,

  portfolio: 200,

  avatar: 500,
};

/* ==========================================================
   URL Fields
========================================================== */

const URL_FIELDS: ProfileField[] = [
  "github",
  "linkedin",
  "portfolio",
  "avatar",
];

/* ==========================================================
   Helpers
========================================================== */

function sanitizeString(
  value: unknown
): string {
  if (
    typeof value !==
    "string"
  ) {
    return "";
  }

  return value.trim();
}

/* ==========================================================
   URL Validation
========================================================== */

function isValidHttpUrl(
  value: string
): boolean {
  if (!value) {
    return true;
  }

  try {
    const normalized =
      /^https?:\/\//i.test(
        value
      )
        ? value
        : `https://${value}`;

    const url =
      new URL(normalized);

    return (
      url.protocol ===
        "http:" ||
      url.protocol ===
        "https:"
    );
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/* ==========================================================
   Build Safe User Response
========================================================== */

export function buildProfileUserResponse(
  user: {
    _id: unknown;

    name: string;

    email: string;

    isProUser: boolean;

    role: string;

    bio?: string;

    title?: string;

    location?: string;

    phone?: string;

    github?: string;

    linkedin?: string;

    portfolio?: string;

    avatar?: string;
  }
) {
  return {
    id: String(
      user._id
    ),

    name: user.name,

    email: user.email,

    isProUser:
      user.isProUser,

    role: user.role,

    bio:
      user.bio ?? "",

    title:
      user.title ?? "",

    location:
      user.location ?? "",

    phone:
      user.phone ?? "",

    github:
      user.github ?? "",

    linkedin:
      user.linkedin ?? "",

    portfolio:
      user.portfolio ?? "",

    avatar:
      user.avatar ?? "",
  };
}

/* ==========================================================
   Build Profile Update
========================================================== */

function buildProfileUpdate(
  body: ProfileUpdateBody
):
  | {
      update: Partial<
        Record<
          ProfileField,
          string
        >
      >;
      error: string | null;
    } {
  const update: Partial<
    Record<
      ProfileField,
      string
    >
  > = {};

  for (
    const field of PROFILE_FIELDS
  ) {
    if (
      body[field] ===
      undefined
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const value =
      sanitizeString(
        body[field]
      );

    if (
      value.length >
      FIELD_LIMITS[field]
    ) {
      return {
        update: {},

        error:
          `${field} cannot exceed ${FIELD_LIMITS[field]} characters.`,
      };
    }

    update[field] =
      value;
  }

  /* ========================================================
     Name Validation
  ======================================================== */

  if (
    update.name !==
    undefined
  ) {
    if (
      update.name.length <
      2
    ) {
      return {
        update: {},

        error:
          "Name must contain at least 2 characters.",
      };
    }
  }

  /* ========================================================
     URL Validation
  ======================================================== */

  for (
    const field of URL_FIELDS
  ) {
    const value =
      update[field];

    if (
      value &&
      !isValidHttpUrl(
        value
      )
    ) {
      return {
        update: {},

        error:
          `${field} must be a valid HTTP or HTTPS URL.`,
      };
    }
  }

  return {
    update,

    error: null,
  };
}

/* ==========================================================
   CREATE PROFILE
========================================================== */

export async function createProfile(
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const authReq =
      req as AuthRequest;

    const userId =
      authReq.user?.id;

    if (!userId) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Authentication required.",
      });

      return;
    }

    const {
      update,
      error,
    } =
      buildProfileUpdate(
        req.body as ProfileUpdateBody
      );

    if (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,

        message: error,
      });

      return;
    }

    if (
      Object.keys(
        update
      ).length === 0
    ) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "No profile data was provided.",
      });

      return;
    }

    const updatedUser =
      await User.findByIdAndUpdate(
        userId,
        {
          $set: update,
        },
        {
          new: true,

          runValidators: true,
        }
      ).select(
        "-password -refreshToken"
      );

    if (!updatedUser) {
      res.status(404).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "User not found.",
      });

      return;
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,

      message:
        "Profile created successfully.",

      user:
        buildProfileUserResponse(
          updatedUser
        ),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Create Profile Error:",
      error
    );

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,

      message:
        "Unable to create profile.",
    });
  }
}

/* ==========================================================
   UPDATE PROFILE
========================================================== */

export async function updateProfile(
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const authReq =
      req as AuthRequest;

    const userId =
      authReq.user?.id;

    if (!userId) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Authentication required.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Build Safe Update
    ====================================================== */

    const {
      update,
      error,
    } =
      buildProfileUpdate(
        req.body as ProfileUpdateBody
      );

    if (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,

        message: error,
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Prevent Empty Update
    ====================================================== */

    if (
      Object.keys(
        update
      ).length === 0
    ) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "No profile changes were provided.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Atomic Update
    ====================================================== */

    const updatedUser =
      await User.findOneAndUpdate(
        {
          _id: userId,
        },
        {
          $set: update,
        },
        {
          new: true,

          runValidators: true,

          context: "query",
        }
      ).select(
        "-password -refreshToken"
      );

    if (!updatedUser) {
      res.status(404).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "User not found.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Response
    ====================================================== */

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,

      message:
        "Profile updated successfully.",

      user:
        buildProfileUserResponse(
          updatedUser
        ),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Update Profile Error:",
      error
    );

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,

      message:
        "Unable to update profile.",
    });
  }
}

import { Request, Response } from "express";
import { logAdminAction } from "../../middleware/admin.middleware.js";
import AdminSettings, { getOrCreateAdminSettings } from "../../models/admin-settings.model.js";

export const getAIOverview = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const settings = await getOrCreateAdminSettings();
    const overview = {
      totalRequests: 1250,
      successfulRequests: 1240,
      failedRequests: 10,
      averageResponseTime: 420,
      tokensUsed: 450000,
      estimatedCost: 2.25,
      providers: settings.providerSettings || {},
      recentActivity: [],
    };

    await logAdminAction(req, "settings_updated", "system", "Viewed AI overview");
    res.json({ success: true, data: overview });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch AI overview" });
  }
};

export const getAIProviders = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const settings = await getOrCreateAdminSettings();
    const providerObj = settings.providerSettings || {};

    const providers = Object.entries(providerObj).map(([key, val]: [string, any]) => ({
      id: key,
      name: key.toUpperCase(),
      status: val?.enabled ? "active" : "inactive",
      usage: val?.maxRequestsPerHour || 100
    }));

    await logAdminAction(req, "settings_updated", "system", "Viewed AI providers");
    res.json({ success: true, data: providers });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch AI providers" });
  }
};

export const updateAIProvider = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const providerParam = req.params.provider;
    const provider = Array.isArray(providerParam) ? providerParam[0] : providerParam;
    const { status } = req.body; // 'active' or 'inactive'
    const enabled = status === "active";

    if (provider) {
      await AdminSettings.findOneAndUpdate({}, { $set: { [`providerSettings.${provider}.enabled`]: enabled } });
    }

    await logAdminAction(req, "provider_configured", provider || "unknown", `Updated AI provider status: ${status}`);
    res.json({ success: true, message: "AI provider updated successfully" });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to update AI provider" });
  }
};

import { Request, Response } from "express";
import { logAdminAction } from "../../middleware/admin.middleware.js";
import User from "../../models/user.model.js";
import Subscription from "../../models/subscription.model.js";

export const getAnalyticsOverview = async (req: Request, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const totalUsers = await User.countDocuments();
    const activeUsers = await User.countDocuments({ status: { $ne: "suspended" } });
    const inactiveUsers = totalUsers - activeUsers;

    const activeSubs = await Subscription.countDocuments({ status: "active" });
    const expiredSubs = await Subscription.countDocuments({ status: "expired" });
    const cancelledSubs = await Subscription.countDocuments({ status: "cancelled" });

    const analyticsData = {
      users: {
        total: totalUsers,
        active: activeUsers,
        inactive: inactiveUsers,
      },
      aiUsage: {
        today: 145,
        thisWeek: 980,
        thisMonth: 4200,
        total: 15600,
      },
      subscriptions: {
        active: activeSubs,
        expired: expiredSubs,
        cancelled: cancelledSubs,
      },
    };

    await logAdminAction(req, "settings_updated", "analytics", "Viewed analytics overview");
    res.json({ success: true, data: analyticsData });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch analytics" });
  }
};

import { Request, Response } from "express";
import { logAdminAction } from "../../middleware/admin.middleware.js";
import Template from "../../models/template.model.js";

export const getAllTemplates = async (req: Request, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const templates = await Template.find().sort({ createdAt: -1 });
    const formatted = templates.map((t) => ({
      id: t._id.toString(),
      name: t.name,
      type: t.type,
      createdAt: t.createdAt,
      updatedAt: t.updatedAt,
    }));

    await logAdminAction(req, "settings_updated", "templates", "Viewed templates");
    res.json({ success: true, data: formatted });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch templates" });
  }
};

export const createTemplate = async (req: Request, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const name = req.body.name || (req.file ? req.file.originalname : "Default Template");
    const type = req.body.type || "Resume";
    const fileUrl = req.file ? `/uploads/${req.file.filename}` : "";

    const newTemplate = await Template.create({ name, type, fileUrl });

    await logAdminAction(req, "template_created", newTemplate._id.toString(), `Created template: ${name}`);
    res.json({ success: true, message: "Template created successfully", data: newTemplate });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to create template" });
  }
};

export const deleteTemplate = async (req: Request, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const idParam = req.params.id;
    const id = Array.isArray(idParam) ? idParam[0] : idParam;

    await Template.findByIdAndDelete(id);

    await logAdminAction(req, "template_updated", id || "unknown", "Deleted template");
    res.json({ success: true, message: "Template deleted successfully" });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to delete template" });
  }
};

import User from "../../models/user.model.js";
import Subscription from "../../models/subscription.model.js";
import AdminUsage from "../../models/admin-usage.model.js";
import AdminSettings from "../../models/admin-settings.model.js";
import { logAdminAction } from "../../middleware/admin.middleware.js";
import { Request, Response } from "express";

export const getAdminDashboard = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    // Get basic stats for dashboard
    const userCount = await User.countDocuments();
    const proUserCount = await User.countDocuments({ isProUser: true });
    const adminCount = await User.countDocuments({ role: "admin" });

    // Get subscription distribution
    const subscriptionStats = await Subscription.aggregate([
      { $group: { _id: "$plan", count: { $sum: 1 } } }
    ]);

    // Get recent user activity (last 7 days)
    const lastWeek = new Date();
    lastWeek.setDate(lastWeek.getDate() - 7);

    const recentUsers = await User.countDocuments({
      createdAt: { $gte: lastWeek }
    });

    // Get AI usage stats (if available)
    const aiStats = await getAIStats();

    // Get recent admin actions for activity log
    const recentAdminActions = await AdminUsage.find()
      .populate("adminId", "name email")
      .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
      .limit(5);

    // Get admin settings for quick overview
    const adminSettings = await AdminSettings.findOne();

    await logAdminAction(req, "settings_updated", "system", "Viewed admin dashboard");

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        users: {
          total: userCount,
          proUsers: proUserCount,
          admins: adminCount,
          newThisWeek: recentUsers,
        },
        subscriptions: subscriptionStats,
        ai: aiStats,
        systemHealth: {
          status: "healthy",
          uptime: process.uptime ? Math.floor(process.uptime() / 3600) : 0,
          lastBackup: await getLastBackupInfo(),
          activeConnections: 0,
        },
        recentAdminActions: recentAdminActions.map(action => ({
          id: action._id.toString(),
          admin: typeof action.adminId === 'object' && action.adminId ? (action.adminId as any).name || "System" : "System",
          action: action.action,
          target: action.target,
          details: action.details,
          createdAt: action.createdAt,
        })),
        adminSettings: {
          maintenanceMode: adminSettings?.system?.maintenanceMode || false,
          require2FA: adminSettings?.security?.require2FA || false,
          analyticsEnabled: adminSettings?.system?.analyticsEnabled || true,
          activeProviders: Object.entries(adminSettings?.providerSettings || {})
            .filter(([_, v]: [string, any]) => v?.enabled)
            .map(([k]) => k),
        },
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch dashboard data" });
  }
};

async function getAIStats() {
  return {
    totalRequests: 1250,
    successfulRequests: 1240,
    failedRequests: 10,
    providers: ["gemini", "deepseek", "qwen"],
  };
}

async function getLastBackupInfo() {
  return {
    lastBackup: new Date(Date.now() - 86400000).toISOString(),
    nextBackup: new Date(Date.now() + 86400000).toISOString(),
  };
}

import User from "../../models/user.model.js";
import Subscription from "../../models/subscription.model.js";
import { logAdminAction } from "../../middleware/admin.middleware.js";
import { Request, Response } from "express";

export const getAllUsers = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const page = parseInt(req.query.page as string) || 1;
    const limit = parseInt(req.query.limit as string) || 10;
    const skip = (page - 1) * limit;
    const search = req.query.search as string;
    const role = req.query.role as string;
    const status = req.query.status as string;

    const filter: any = {};
    if (search) {
      filter.$or = [
        { name: { $regex: search, $options: "i" } },
        { email: { $regex: search, $options: "i" } }
      ];
    }
    if (role) {
      filter.role = role;
    }
    if (status) {
      filter.status = status;
    }

    const users = await User.find(filter)
      .select("-password -refreshToken")
      .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
      .skip(skip)
      .limit(limit);

    const total = await User.countDocuments(filter);

    await logAdminAction(req, "user_updated", "all", `Viewed all users (page ${page})`);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: users,
      pagination: {
        page,
        limit,
        total,
        pages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch users" });
  }
};

export const getUserDetails = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const idParam = req.params.id;
    const id = Array.isArray(idParam) ? idParam[0] : idParam;

    const user = await User.findById(id).select("-password -refreshToken");
    if (!user) {
      res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: "User not found" });
      return;
    }

    const subscription = await Subscription.findOne({ user: id });
    const aiUsage = await getUserAIUsage(id);

    await logAdminAction(req, "user_updated", id, `Viewed user details`);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        user,
        subscription,
        aiUsage
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch user details" });
  }
};

export const updateUser = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const idParam = req.params.id;
    const id = Array.isArray(idParam) ? idParam[0] : idParam;
    const updateData = req.body;

    const user = await User.findByIdAndUpdate(
      id,
      { $set: updateData },
      { new: true }
    ).select("-password -refreshToken");

    if (!user) {
      res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: "User not found" });
      return;
    }

    await logAdminAction(req, "user_updated", id, `Updated user: ${JSON.stringify(updateData)}`);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: user
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to update user" });
  }
};

export const updateUserStatus = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const idParam = req.params.id;
    const id = Array.isArray(idParam) ? idParam[0] : idParam;
    const { status } = req.body; // 'active' or 'suspended'

    const user = await User.findByIdAndUpdate(
      id,
      { $set: { status } },
      { new: true }
    ).select("-password -refreshToken");

    if (!user) {
      res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: "User not found" });
      return;
    }

    await logAdminAction(req, "user_updated", id, `Updated user status to ${status}`);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: user,
      message: `User ${status === "suspended" ? "suspended" : "activated"} successfully`
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to update user status" });
  }
};

export const deleteUser = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    const idParam = req.params.id;
    const id = Array.isArray(idParam) ? idParam[0] : idParam;

    const user = await User.findById(id);
    if (!user) {
      res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: "User not found" });
      return;
    }

    await User.findByIdAndDelete(id);

    await logAdminAction(req, "user_deleted", id, `Deleted user account`);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: "User deleted successfully"
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to delete user" });
  }
};

    today: 0,

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\controllers\auth\refresh-token.controller.ts

============================================================

import type {
  Request,
  Response,
} from "express";

import jwt from "jsonwebtoken";

import User from "../../models/user.model.js";

import generateToken from "../../utils/generateToken.js";

/* ==========================================================
   JWT Payload
========================================================== */

interface RefreshTokenPayload {
  userId: string;

  iat?: number;

  exp?: number;
}

/* ==========================================================
   Refresh Secret
========================================================== */

function getRefreshSecret(): string {
  const secret =
    process.env.JWT_REFRESH_SECRET;

  if (
    typeof secret !== "string" ||
    secret.trim().length < 32
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "JWT_REFRESH_SECRET must be configured with at least 32 characters."
    );
  }

  return secret.trim();
}

/* ==========================================================
   Refresh Token Controller
========================================================== */

export async function refreshTokenController(
  req: Request,
  res: Response
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    /* ======================================================
       Read Refresh Cookie
    ====================================================== */

    const refreshToken =
      req.cookies?.refreshToken;

    if (
      typeof refreshToken !==
        "string" ||
      refreshToken.trim().length ===
        0
    ) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Refresh token missing.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Verify JWT
    ====================================================== */

    let decoded: RefreshTokenPayload;

    try {
      decoded =
        jwt.verify(
          refreshToken,
          getRefreshSecret()
        ) as RefreshTokenPayload;
    } catch {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Invalid or expired refresh token.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Validate Payload
    ====================================================== */

    if (
      typeof decoded.userId !==
        "string" ||
      decoded.userId.trim().length ===
        0
    ) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Invalid refresh token.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Find User
    ====================================================== */

    const user =
      await User.findById(
        decoded.userId
      ).select(
        "+refreshToken"
      );

    if (!user) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Invalid refresh token.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Validate Stored Refresh Token
    ====================================================== */

    if (
      typeof user.refreshToken !==
        "string" ||
      user.refreshToken !==
        refreshToken
    ) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Refresh token is no longer valid.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Rotate Tokens
    ====================================================== */

    const tokens =
      generateToken(
        res,
        user._id.toString()
      );

    /* ======================================================
       Persist New Refresh Token
    ====================================================== */

    user.refreshToken =
      tokens.refreshToken;

    await user.save();

    /* ======================================================
       Response
    ====================================================== */

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,

      message:
        "Access token refreshed successfully.",

      token:
        tokens.accessToken,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Refresh token controller error:",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    res.status(401).json({
      success: false,

      message:
        "Unable to refresh authentication.",
    });
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\interview\competency\ai-competency-generator.ts

============================================================

import type {
  Competency,
  DifficultyLevel,
} from "./competency-types.js";
import type {
  InterviewDifficulty,
  InterviewLanguage,
  InterviewType,
} from "../types/interview.types.js";

export interface AICompetenciesInput {
  userId: string;
  profession: string;
  skill: string;
  interviewType: InterviewType;
  difficulty: InterviewDifficulty;
  language: InterviewLanguage;
}

export async function generateAICompetencies(
  input: AICompetenciesInput
): Promise<Competency[]> {
  const diff: DifficultyLevel =
    input.difficulty === "beginner"
      ? "beginner"
      : input.difficulty === "advanced"
      ? "advanced"
      : "intermediate";

  return [
    {
      id: `${input.skill.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-")}-core`,
      name: `Core ${input.skill} Concepts`,
      description: `Fundamental understanding and practical application of ${input.skill}.`,
      difficulty: diff,
      tags: [input.skill],
      keywords: [input.skill.toLowerCase()],
    },
  ];
}



============================================================

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\interview\competency\competency-engine.ts

============================================================

// server/src/interview/competency/competency-engine.ts

import {
  COMPETENCY_MAP,
} from "./competency-map.js";

import type {
  Competency,
  CompetencySelectionResult,
} from "./competency-types.js";

import {
  generateAICompetencies,
} from "./ai-competency-generator.js";

import type {
  InterviewType,
  InterviewDifficulty,
  InterviewLanguage,
} from "../types/interview.types.js";

export interface CompetencyEngineInput {
  userId: string;

  profession: string;

  interviewType: InterviewType;

  difficulty: InterviewDifficulty;

  language: InterviewLanguage;

  skills: string[];

  coveredCompetencies?: string[];
}

export class CompetencyEngine {
  static async getNextCompetency({
    userId,
    profession,
    interviewType,
    difficulty,
    language,
    skills,
    coveredCompetencies = [],
  }: CompetencyEngineInput): Promise<
    CompetencySelectionResult | null
  > {
    if (
      !Array.isArray(skills) ||
      skills.length === 0
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    const normalizedCovered =
      new Set(
        coveredCompetencies
          .filter(Boolean)
          .map((id) =>
            id.trim().toLowerCase()
          )
      );

    for (const rawSkill of skills) {
      const skill =
        rawSkill
          ?.toLowerCase()
          .trim();

      if (!skill) {
        continue;
      }

      let competencies: Competency[] =
        [];

      const map =
        COMPETENCY_MAP[skill];

      /*
       * Static competency map has priority.
       */
      if (map) {
        competencies =
          map.competencies.filter(
            (competency) =>
              competency.difficulty ===
              difficulty
          );
      } else {
        /*
         * Unknown skill -> AI generation.
         *
         * Do not silently swallow the error.
         */
        try {
          competencies =
            await generateAICompetencies({
              userId,

              profession,

              skill,

              interviewType,

              difficulty,

              language,
            });
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(
            `[Interview][CompetencyEngine] Failed to generate competencies for skill "${skill}":`,
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : error
          );

          /*
           * Try the next skill instead of
           * immediately killing the interview.
           */
          continue;
        }
      }

      if (!competencies.length) {
        continue;
      }

      const remaining =
        competencies.filter(
          (competency) =>
            !normalizedCovered.has(
              competency.id
                .trim()
                .toLowerCase()
            )
        );

      return {
        currentSkill:
          skill,

        availableCompetencies:
          competencies,
 
        coveredCompetencies:
          competencies
            .filter((competency) =>
              normalizedCovered.has(
                competency.id
                  .trim()
                  .toLowerCase()
              )
            )
            .map(
              (competency) => ({
                id:
                  competency.id,

                name:
                  competency.name,
              })
            ),

        nextCompetency:
          remaining.length > 0
            ? remaining[0]
            : undefined,
      };
    }

    return null;
  }

  static hasStaticCompetency(
    skill: string
  ): boolean {
    return Boolean(
      COMPETENCY_MAP[
        skill
          .toLowerCase()
          .trim()
      ]
    );
  }

  static getCompetencies(
    skill: string
  ): Competency[] {
    return (
      COMPETENCY_MAP[
        skill
          .toLowerCase()
          .trim()
      ]?.competencies ?? []
    );
  }
}

  | "beginner"
  | "intermediate"
  | "advanced";

  name: string;

  keywords: string[];

}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\interview\feedback\communication-scorer.ts

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "../history/interview-history.types.js";

import type {
  CommunicationScore,
} from "./feedback-types.js";

export class CommunicationScorer {
  static score(
    history: InterviewHistoryItem[]
  ): CommunicationScore {
    if (history.length === 0) {
      return {
        score: 0,
        grammar: 0,
        clarity: 0,
        professionalism: 0,
        vocabulary: 0,
      };
    }

    let total = 0;

    for (const item of history) {
      total += item.score ?? 0;
    }

    const average =
      total / history.length;

    return {
      score: average,

      grammar:
        Math.min(
          average + 5,
          100
        ),

      clarity:
        Math.min(
          average + 2,
          100
        ),

      professionalism:
        average,

      vocabulary:
        Math.min(
          average + 4,
          100
        ),
    };
  }
}

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "../history/interview-history.types.js";

import type {
  CompetencyScore,
} from "./feedback-types.js";

export class CompetencyScorer {
  static score(
    history: InterviewHistoryItem[]
  ): CompetencyScore[] {
    return history.map((item) => ({
      competencyId:
        item.competencyId ?? "default-competency",

      competencyName:
        item.competencyName ?? item.skill,

      skill:
        item.skill,

      score:
        item.score ?? 0,

      strengths:
        item.strengths ?? [],

      weaknesses:
        item.weaknesses ?? [],
    }));
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\interview\feedback\confidence-scorer.ts

============================================================

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "../history/interview-history.types.js";

import type {
  ConfidenceScore,
} from "./feedback-types.js";

export class ConfidenceScorer {

  static score(
    history: InterviewHistoryItem[]
  ): ConfidenceScore {

    if (
      history.length === 0
    ) {

      return {

        score: 0,

        confidence: 0,

        hesitation: 0,

        explanationDepth: 0,

      };

    }

    let confidenceTotal = 0;

    let hesitationTotal = 0;

    let explanationDepthTotal = 0;

    for (const item of history) {

      const answer =
        item.answer ?? "";

      const wordCount =
        answer
          .trim()
          .split(/\s+/)
          .filter(Boolean)
          .length;

      // Explanation Depth
      if (wordCount >= 80) {

        explanationDepthTotal += 95;

      } else if (wordCount >= 50) {

        explanationDepthTotal += 80;

      } else if (wordCount >= 25) {

        explanationDepthTotal += 65;

      } else {

        explanationDepthTotal += 45;

      }

      // Hesitation Detection
      const hesitationWords = [

        "maybe",

        "perhaps",

        "i think",

        "not sure",

        "probably",

        "umm",

        "uh",

        "guess",

      ];

      let hesitationScore = 100;

      const lowerAnswer =
        answer.toLowerCase();

      for (const word of hesitationWords) {

        if (
          lowerAnswer.includes(word)
        ) {

          hesitationScore -= 10;

        }

      }

      hesitationScore =
        Math.max(
          hesitationScore,
          20
        );

      hesitationTotal +=
        hesitationScore;

      // Confidence Score
      confidenceTotal +=
        (
          hesitationScore +
          (
            wordCount >= 40
              ? 90
              : 65
          )
        ) / 2;

    }

    const confidence =
      Math.round(
        confidenceTotal /
          history.length
      );

    const hesitation =
      Math.round(
        hesitationTotal /
          history.length
      );

    const explanationDepth =
      Math.round(
        explanationDepthTotal /
          history.length
      );

    return {

      score: Math.round(
        (
          confidence +
          explanationDepth
        ) / 2
      ),

      confidence,

      hesitation,

      explanationDepth,

    };

  }

}


============================================================

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\interview\feedback\feedback-engine.ts

============================================================

import {
  InterviewValidator,
} from "../validation/interview-validator.js";

import type {
  FeedbackAnalysis,
  FeedbackEngineInput,
} from "./feedback-types.js";

import type {
  CompetencyScore,
} from "./feedback-types.js";

import {
  CommunicationScorer,
} from "./communication-scorer.js";

import {
  CompetencyScorer,
} from "./competency-scorer.js";

import {
  TechnicalScorer,
} from "./technical-scorer.js";

import {
  ConfidenceScorer,
} from "./confidence-scorer.js";

import {
  RecommendationEngine,
} from "./recommendation-engine.js";

import {
  RecruiterVerdictEngine,
} from "./recruiter-verdict.js";

export async function
analyzeInterview(
  input: FeedbackEngineInput
) {

  const {
    history,
  } = input;

  // ==========================
// Interview Validation
// ==========================

const validationResult =
  InterviewValidator.validate(
    history
  );

if (
  !validationResult.valid
) {
  throw new Error(
    validationResult.errors.join(
      "\n"
    )
  );
}

  if (
    history.length === 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Interview history is empty."
    );
  }

 
// ==========================
// Competency Analysis
// ==========================

const competencyScores =
  CompetencyScorer.score(
    history
  );

// ==========================
// Communication Analysis
// ==========================

const communication =
  CommunicationScorer.score(
    history
  );

// ==========================
// Technical Analysis
// ==========================

const technical =
  TechnicalScorer.score(
    history
  );

// ==========================
// Confidence Analysis
// ==========================

const confidence =
  ConfidenceScorer.score(
    history
  );

// ==========================
// Overall Score
// ==========================

const overallScore =
  Math.round(
    (
      technical.score +
      communication.score +
      confidence.score
    ) / 3
  );

  const strengths =
  competencyScores.flatMap(
    (
      competency: CompetencyScore
    ) => competency.strengths
  );

const weaknesses =
  competencyScores.flatMap(
    (
      competency: CompetencyScore
    ) => competency.weaknesses
  );

 const recommendations =
  RecommendationEngine.generate({

    competencyScores,

    communication,

    confidence,

    technical,

    strengths,

    weaknesses,

    recommendations: [],

    recruiterVerdict: "",

    overallScore,

  });

const recruiterSummary =
  RecruiterVerdictEngine.evaluate({

    competencyScores,

    communication,

    confidence,

    technical,

    strengths,

    weaknesses,

    recommendations,

    recruiterVerdict: "",

    overallScore,

  });



  const analysis: FeedbackAnalysis = {

  competencyScores,

  communication,

  confidence,

  technical,

  strengths,

  weaknesses,

  recommendations,

  recruiterVerdict:
    recruiterSummary.summary,

  recruiterSummary,

  overallScore,

};

return analysis;
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\interview\feedback\recommendation-engine.ts

import type {
  FeedbackAnalysis,
} from "./feedback-types.js";

export interface RecommendationItem {
  title: string;

  description: string;

  priority: "high" | "medium" | "low";

  category:
    | "technical"
    | "communication"
    | "confidence"
    | "career";
}

export class RecommendationEngine {

  static generate(
    analysis: FeedbackAnalysis
  ): RecommendationItem[] {

    const recommendations: RecommendationItem[] = [];

    // ==========================
    // Technical
    // ==========================

    if (
      analysis.overallScore < 70
    ) {

      recommendations.push({

        title:
          "Strengthen Technical Fundamentals",

        description:
          "Spend time revising core concepts and solve more practical coding problems.",

        priority: "high",

        category: "technical",

      });

    }

    // ==========================
    // Communication
    // ==========================

    if (
      analysis.communication.score <
      75
    ) {

      recommendations.push({

        title:
          "Improve Communication",

        description:
          "Practice explaining your solutions clearly and professionally during interviews.",

        priority: "medium",

        category: "communication",

      });

    }

    // ==========================
    // Confidence
    // ==========================

    if (
      analysis.confidence.score <
      75
    ) {

      recommendations.push({

        title:
          "Increase Interview Confidence",

        description:
          "Practice mock interviews and answer questions without hesitation.",

        priority: "medium",

        category: "confidence",

      });

    }

    // ==========================
    // Weak Competencies
    // ==========================

    analysis.competencyScores.forEach(
      (
        competency
      ) => {

        if (
          competency.score < 70
        ) {

          recommendations.push({

            title:
              `Improve ${competency.competencyName}`,

            description:
              `Focus on strengthening your ${competency.skill} knowledge through projects and consistent practice.`,

            priority: "high",

            category: "technical",

          });

        }

      }
    );

    // ==========================
    // Career Recommendation
    // ==========================

    if (
      analysis.overallScore >= 85
    ) {

      recommendations.push({

        title:
          "Apply for Product-Based Companies",

        description:
          "Your interview performance indicates readiness for high-quality product engineering roles.",

        priority: "low",

        category: "career",

      });

    }

    return recommendations;

  }

}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\interview\feedback\recruiter-verdict.ts

============================================================

import type {
  FeedbackAnalysis,
} from "./feedback-types.js";

export interface RecruiterVerdict {

  verdict:
    | "Strong Hire"
    | "Hire"
    | "Borderline"
    | "No Hire";

  hireScore: number;

  summary: string;

  positives: string[];

  concerns: string[];

}

export class RecruiterVerdictEngine {

  static evaluate(
    analysis: FeedbackAnalysis
  ): RecruiterVerdict {

    const positives: string[] = [];

    const concerns: string[] = [];

    // ==========================
    // Technical
    // ==========================

    if (
      analysis.overallScore >= 80
    ) {

      positives.push(
        "Strong technical performance."
      );

    } else {

      concerns.push(
        "Technical performance requires improvement."
      );

    }

    // ==========================
    // Communication
    // ==========================

    if (
      analysis.communication.score >= 75
    ) {

      positives.push(
        "Professional communication."
      );

    } else {

      concerns.push(
        "Communication skills need improvement."
      );

    }

    // ==========================
    // Confidence
    // ==========================

    if (
      analysis.confidence.score >= 75
    ) {

      positives.push(
        "Confident during interview."
      );

    } else {

      concerns.push(
        "Candidate showed hesitation."
      );

    }

    // ==========================
    // Competencies
    // ==========================

    const weakCompetencies =
      analysis.competencyScores.filter(
        (
          competency
        ) =>
          competency.score < 70
      );

    if (
      weakCompetencies.length > 0
    ) {

      concerns.push(
        `${weakCompetencies.length} competency areas require improvement.`
      );

    }

    // ==========================
    // Final Verdict
    // ==========================

    let verdict:
      RecruiterVerdict["verdict"];

    let summary: string;

    if (
      analysis.overallScore >= 90
    ) {

      verdict = "Strong Hire";

      summary =
        "Outstanding interview performance with excellent technical knowledge and communication.";

    } else if (
      analysis.overallScore >= 80
    ) {

      verdict = "Hire";

      summary =
        "Good interview performance. Candidate is suitable for hiring.";

    } else if (
      analysis.overallScore >= 65
    ) {

      verdict =
        "Borderline";

      summary =
        "Candidate shows potential but requires improvement before hiring.";

    } else {

      verdict =
        "No Hire";

      summary =
        "Interview performance does not currently meet hiring expectations.";

    }

    return {

      verdict,

      hireScore:
        analysis.overallScore,

      summary,

      positives,

      concerns,

    };

  }

}

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "../history/interview-history.types.js";

import type {
  TechnicalScore,
} from "./feedback-types.js";


export class TechnicalScorer {

  static score(
    history: InterviewHistoryItem[]
  ): TechnicalScore {

    if (
      history.length === 0
    ) {

      return {

        score: 0,

        problemSolving: 0,

        codeQuality: 0,

        accuracy: 0,

        conceptUnderstanding: 0,

      };

    }

    let totalScore = 0;

for (const item of history) {

  totalScore +=
    item.score ?? 0;

}

const averageScore =
  totalScore /
  history.length;

const problemSolving =
  Math.min(
    averageScore + 3,
    100
  );

const codeQuality =
  Math.min(
    averageScore + 5,
    100
  );

const accuracy =
  Math.min(
    averageScore + 2,
    100
  );

const conceptUnderstanding =
  Math.min(
    averageScore + 4,
    100
  );


  return {

  score: Math.round(
    averageScore
  ),

  problemSolving,

  codeQuality,

  accuracy,

  conceptUnderstanding,

};

  }

}

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "./interview-history.types.js";

export interface UpdateHistoryAnswerInput {
  answer: string;
}

export interface UpdateHistoryEvaluationInput {
  score?: number;

  feedback?: string;

  strengths?: string[];

  weaknesses?: string[];
}

export interface UpdateHistoryMetadataInput {
  provider?: string;

  model?: string;
}

export class InterviewHistoryUpdater {
  /**
   * Updates candidate answer.
   */
  static updateAnswer(
    item: InterviewHistoryItem,
    input: UpdateHistoryAnswerInput
  ): InterviewHistoryItem {
    return {
      ...item,

      answer: input.answer.trim(),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Updates AI evaluation.
   */
  static updateEvaluation(
    item: InterviewHistoryItem,
    input: UpdateHistoryEvaluationInput
  ): InterviewHistoryItem {
    return {
      ...item,

      score:
        input.score ?? item.score,

      feedback:
        input.feedback ?? item.feedback,

      strengths:
        input.strengths ??
        item.strengths,

      weaknesses:
        input.weaknesses ??
        item.weaknesses,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Updates AI provider metadata.
   */
  static updateMetadata(
    item: InterviewHistoryItem,
    input: UpdateHistoryMetadataInput
  ): InterviewHistoryItem {
    return {
      ...item,

      provider:
        input.provider ??
        item.provider,

      model:
        input.model ??
        item.model,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Generic updater.
   * Future-safe for new fields.
   */
  static merge(
    item: InterviewHistoryItem,
    updates: Partial<InterviewHistoryItem>
  ): InterviewHistoryItem {
    return {
      ...item,
      ...updates,
    };
  }
}

/* =========================================================
   CareerBoost AI
   Adaptive Interview â€” Canonical Types
========================================================= */

/* =========================================================
   Interview Type
========================================================= */

/**
 * Supported adaptive interview modes.
 *
 * skill:
 * Technical / domain-specific practical interview.
 *
 * hr:
 * Behavioral, leadership and HR interview.
 *
 * mixed:
 * Adaptive combination of technical, behavioral,
 * situational and problem-solving questions.
 *
 
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * Keep this as the single source of truth for the
 * backend interview type system.
 */
export type InterviewType =
  | "skill"
  | "hr"
  | "mixed";

/* =========================================================
   Interview Difficulty
========================================================= */

export type InterviewDifficulty =
  | "beginner"
  | "intermediate"
  | "advanced";

/* =========================================================
   Interview Language
========================================================= */

export type InterviewLanguage =
  | "english"
  | "hindi"
  | "hinglish"
  | "urdu";

/* =========================================================
   Interview Question Type
========================================================= */

export type InterviewQuestionType =
  | "skill"
  | "behavioral"
  | "situational"
  | "hr";



/* =========================================================
   Runtime Constants
========================================================= */

export const INTERVIEW_TYPES = [
  "skill",
  "hr",
  "mixed",
] as const;

export const INTERVIEW_DIFFICULTIES = [
  "beginner",
  "intermediate",
  "advanced",
] as const;

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\interview\validation\interview-validator.ts

============================================================

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "../history/interview-history.types.js";

export interface ValidationResult {

  valid: boolean;

  errors: string[];

}


// ==========================================
// Interview Validation Engine
// ==========================================

export class InterviewValidator {

  static validate(
    history: InterviewHistoryItem[]
  ): ValidationResult {

    const errors: string[] = [];

    // ==========================
    // Empty History
    // ==========================

    if (history.length === 0) {

      errors.push(
        "Interview history cannot be empty."
      );

      return {
        valid: false,
        errors,
      };

    }

    // ==========================
    // Validate Every Item
    // ==========================

    history.forEach(
      (item, index) => {

        const row =
          `History Item ${index + 1}`;

        // Question

        if (
          !item.question?.trim()
        ) {
          errors.push(
            `${row}: Question is required.`
          );
        }

        // Answer

        if (
          !item.answer?.trim()
        ) {
          errors.push(
            `${row}: Answer is required.`
          );
        }

        // Skill

        if (
          !item.skill?.trim()
        ) {
          errors.push(
            `${row}: Skill is required.`
          );
        }

        // Competency Id

        if (
          !item.competencyId?.trim()
        ) {
          errors.push(
            `${row}: Competency ID is required.`
          );
        }

        // Competency Name

        if (
          !item.competencyName?.trim()
        ) {
          errors.push(
            `${row}: Competency name is required.`
          );
        }

        // Difficulty

        if (
          ![
            "beginner",
            "intermediate",
            "advanced",
          ].includes(
            item.difficulty
          )
        ) {
          errors.push(
            `${row}: Invalid difficulty level.`
          );
        }

        // Score Validation

        if (
          item.score !==
          undefined
        ) {

          if (
            Number.isNaN(
              item.score
            )
          ) {

            errors.push(
              `${row}: Score must be numeric.`
            );

          } else if (

            item.score < 0 ||

            item.score > 100

          ) {

            errors.push(
              `${row}: Score must be between 0 and 100.`
            );

          }

        }

      }
    );

    return {

      valid:
        errors.length === 0,

      errors,

    };

  }

}

import type {
  InterviewHistoryItem,
} from "../history/interview-history.types.js";

const STOP_WORDS = new Set([
  "what",
  "is",
  "are",
  "the",
  "a",
  "an",
  "of",
  "to",
  "can",
  "you",
  "please",
  "explain",
  "describe",
  "tell",
  "about",
  "how",
  "why",
  "when",
  "where",
  "define",
  "in",
  "for",
  "your",
  "kya",
  "kaise",
  "kyun",
  "hai",
  "hota",
  "hoti",
  "karoge",
  "karenge",
  "agar",
  "basic",
]);

function normalize(text: string): string[] {
  return text
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^\w\s]/g, "")
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(
      (word) =>
        word.length > 0 &&
        !STOP_WORDS.has(word)
    );
}

function similarity(
  a: string[],
  b: string[]
): number {
  const setA = new Set(a);
  const setB = new Set(b);

  const intersection =
    [...setA].filter((word) =>
      setB.has(word)
    );

  const union = new Set([
    ...setA,
    ...setB,
  ]);

  if (union.size === 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  return (
    intersection.length /
    union.size
  );
}

export function isDuplicateQuestion(
  question: string,
  history: InterviewHistoryItem[],
  threshold = 0.55
): boolean {
  const normalizedQuestion =
    normalize(question);

  return history.some((item) => {
    const score = similarity(
      normalizedQuestion,
      normalize(item.question)
    );

    return score >= threshold;
  });
}

import { aiManager } from "./ai-engine.js";

import {
  recordAIProviderSuccess,
} from "./ai-telemetry.service.js";

import {
  assertAIQuota,
  commitAIUsage,
} from "./ai-usage.service.js";

import type {
  AIProviderName,
} from "./types/provider.types.js";

import type {
  AIUsageFeature,
} from "../../constants/ai-usage.constants.js";

/**
 * Application-level AI modules.
 *
 * These represent actual CareerBoost features.
 * They are intentionally different from AIUsageFeature,
 * which represents the canonical quota buckets.
 */
export type AIUsageModule =
  | "general"
  | "career-intelligence"
  | "resume-builder"
  | "resume-analysis"
  | "cover-letter"
  | "interview"
  | "interview-answer"
  | "interview-evaluation"
  | "interview-feedback"
  | "recruiter-simulation"
  | "linkedin-optimizer"
  | "career-insights"
  | "evaluation"
  | "competency"
  | "question-regenerator";

/**
 * Canonical mapping:
 *
 * Application module
 *        â†“
 * AI usage/quota feature
 *
 * Keep this mapping centralized.
 * Do not duplicate this logic inside individual services.
 */
const AI_MODULE_TO_FEATURE: Record<
  AIUsageModule,
  AIUsageFeature
> = {
  general: "general",

  "career-intelligence": "career",
  "career-insights": "career",

  "resume-builder": "resume",

  "resume-analysis": "ats",

  "cover-letter": "coverLetter",

  "linkedin-optimizer": "linkedin",

  "recruiter-simulation": "recruiter",

  interview: "interview",
  "interview-answer": "interview",
  "interview-evaluation": "interview",
  "interview-feedback": "interview",

  evaluation: "interview",
  competency: "interview",
  "question-regenerator": "interview",
};

/**
 * Converts an application module into
 * the canonical quota feature.
 */
function moduleToFeature(
  module: AIUsageModule
): AIUsageFeature {
  return AI_MODULE_TO_FEATURE[module];
}

/**
 * Input accepted by the centralized AI orchestrator.
 */
export interface AIOrchestratorInput {
  /**
   * Authenticated application user.
   */
  userId: string;

  /**
   * Application-level AI module.
   *
   * Example:
   *
   * "resume-builder"
   * "career-intelligence"
   * "interview"
   */
  module?: AIUsageModule;

  /**
   * Optional explicit quota feature.
   *
   * Normally this should NOT be required because
   * the orchestrator derives it from `module`.
   *
   * Explicit feature takes priority when supplied.
   */
  feature?: AIUsageFeature;

  /**
   * Prompt sent to the AI provider manager.
   */
  prompt: string;

  /**
   * Optional language hint.
   */
  language?: string;

  /**
   * Optional recruiter-mode flag.
   */
  recruiterMode?: boolean;

  /**
   * Enables centralized quota enforcement.
   *
   * false/undefined:
   *     AI request runs without quota handling.
   *
   * true:
   *     assert quota before provider call
   *     and commit usage after successful response.
   */
  enforceQuota?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Standard response returned by the centralized
 * AI orchestration layer.
 */
export interface AIOrchestratorResponse {
  /**
   * Canonical AI response text.
   */
  text: string;

  /**
   * Parsed JSON if the provider returned structured JSON.
   */
  json?: unknown;

  /**
   * Backward-compatible alias.
   *
   * Existing CareerBoost services currently consume
   * `response.content`.
   */
  content: string;

  /**
   * Provider that successfully generated the response.
   */
  provider: AIProviderName;

  /**
   * Model used by the successful provider.
   */
  model: string;

  /**
   * Total provider latency.
   */
  latency: number;

  /**
   * Number of provider attempts performed
   * by AIManager.
   */
  attempts: number;

  /**
   * Providers attempted during fallback.
   */
  attemptedProviders: AIProviderName[];

  /**
   * Quota information when quota enforcement
   * or quota state is available.
   */
  usage?: {
    used: number;
    limit: number;
    remaining: number;
    resetAt: Date;
  };
}

/**
 * Maximum prompt size accepted by the orchestrator.
 *
 * This prevents accidental oversized requests
 * from reaching the provider layer.
 */
const MAX_PROMPT_LENGTH = 30_000;

/**
 * Validates all mandatory AI request fields.
 */
function validateInput(
  data: AIOrchestratorInput
): void {
  if (!data.userId?.trim()) {
    throw new Error(
      "Authenticated userId is required for AI requests."
    );
  }

  if (!data.prompt?.trim()) {
    throw new Error(
      "AI prompt cannot be empty."
    );
  }

  if (
    data.prompt.length >
    MAX_PROMPT_LENGTH
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `AI prompt exceeds the maximum allowed size of ${MAX_PROMPT_LENGTH} characters.`
    );
  }
}

/**
 * Returns the application module.
 *
 * Existing services that have not yet been migrated
 * can continue working without explicitly providing
 * a module.
 */
function getModule(
  module?: AIUsageModule
): AIUsageModule {
  return module ?? "general";
}

/**
 * Resolves the final quota feature.
 *
 * Priority:
 *
 * 1. Explicit `feature`
 * 2. Module â†’ feature mapping
 */
function getFeature(
  data: AIOrchestratorInput
): AIUsageFeature {
  if (data.feature) {
    return data.feature;
  }

  return moduleToFeature(
    getModule(data.module)
  );
}

/**
 * Main centralized AI execution pipeline.
 *
 * Flow:
 *
 * validate
 *    â†“
 * resolve module
 *    â†“
 * resolve quota feature
 *    â†“
 * optional quota assertion
 *    â†“
 * AIManager
 *    â†“
 * provider success
 *    â†“
 * telemetry
 *    â†“
 * optional quota commit
 *    â†“
 * normalized response
 */
export async function runAI(
  data: AIOrchestratorInput
): Promise<AIOrchestratorResponse> {
  validateInput(data);

  const module =
    getModule(data.module);

  const feature =
    getFeature(data);

  /**
   * Stores quota state when quota checking
   * is enabled.
   */
  let quotaState:
    Awaited<
      ReturnType<typeof assertAIQuota>
    > | undefined;

  /**
   * IMPORTANT:
   *
   * Quota enforcement is opt-in.
   *
   * This prevents double counting while some
   * legacy routes may still have external
   * middleware-based quota protection.
   */
  if (data.enforceQuota) {
    quotaState =
      await assertAIQuota(
        data.userId,
        feature
      );
  }

  const startedAt =
    Date.now();

  try {
    /**
     * AIManager owns:
     *
     * - provider selection
     * - fallback
     * - provider availability
     * - provider execution
     * - provider-level failure handling
     */
    const response =
      await aiManager.generate(
        data.prompt
      );

    /**
     * Prefer provider-reported latency.
     * Fall back to orchestrator measurement.
     */
    const latency =
      response.latency ??
      Date.now() - startedAt;

    /**
     * Record telemetry only after a genuine
     * successful AI response.
     */
    recordAIProviderSuccess(
      response.provider,
      latency
    );

    let usage:
      AIOrchestratorResponse["usage"];

    /**
     * Commit quota ONLY after successful
     * provider generation.
     *
     * Therefore:
     *
     * provider failure
     *     â†“
     * no commit
     *
     * provider success
     *     â†“
     * commit
     */
    if (data.enforceQuota) {
      const committed =
        await commitAIUsage(
          data.userId,
          feature
        );

      usage = {
        used:
          committed.used,

        limit:
          committed.limit,

        remaining:
          committed.remaining,

        resetAt:
          committed.resetAt,
      };
    } else if (quotaState) {
      usage = {
        used:
          quotaState.used,

        limit:
          quotaState.limit,

        remaining:
          quotaState.remaining,

        resetAt:
          quotaState.resetAt,
      };
    }

    /**
     * Normalize the provider response
     * into the public orchestrator contract.
     */
    return {
      text:
        response.text,

      json:
        response.json,

      /**
       * Backward compatibility for existing
       * CareerBoost services.
       */
      content:
        response.text,

      provider:
        response.provider,

      model:
        response.model,

      latency,

      attempts:
        response.attempts,

      attemptedProviders:
        response.attemptedProviders,

      usage,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    /**
     * Provider fallback and provider-specific
     * failure tracking belong to AIManager.
     *
     * Do not fabricate provider information here.
     */
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Backward-compatible public API.
 *
 * Existing services can continue using:
 *
 * await askAI({
 *   userId,
 *   prompt,
 * });
 *
 * New services should provide:
 *
 * await askAI({
 *   userId,
 *   module: "resume-builder",
 *   prompt,
 *   enforceQuota: true,
 * });
 */
export async function askAI(
  data: AIOrchestratorInput
): Promise<AIOrchestratorResponse> {
  return runAI(data);
}

import type {
  AIProviderName,
} from "./types/provider.types.js";

export interface AIProviderTelemetry {

  provider:
    AIProviderName;

  requests: number;

  successfulRequests: number;

  failedRequests: number;

  totalResponseTime: number;

  averageResponseTime: number;

  successRate: number;

  lastRequestAt?: Date;

  lastSuccessAt?: Date;

  lastFailureAt?: Date;
}

const telemetry =
  new Map<
    AIProviderName,
    AIProviderTelemetry
  >();

function getOrCreate(
  provider: AIProviderName
): AIProviderTelemetry {

  const existing =
    telemetry.get(provider);

  if (existing) {
    return existing;
  }

  const state: AIProviderTelemetry = {
    provider,

    requests: 0,

    successfulRequests: 0,

    failedRequests: 0,

    totalResponseTime: 0,

    averageResponseTime: 0,

    successRate: 100,
  };

  telemetry.set(
    provider,
    state
  );

  return state;
}

export function recordAIProviderSuccess(
  provider: AIProviderName,
  responseTime: number
): void {

  const state =
    getOrCreate(provider);

  state.requests += 1;

  state.successfulRequests += 1;

  state.totalResponseTime +=
    responseTime;

  state.averageResponseTime =
    state.totalResponseTime /
    state.requests;

  state.successRate =
    (
      state.successfulRequests /
      state.requests
    ) * 100;

  state.lastRequestAt =
    new Date();

  state.lastSuccessAt =
    new Date();
}

export function recordAIProviderFailure(
  provider: AIProviderName,
  responseTime = 0
): void {

  const state =
    getOrCreate(provider);

  state.requests += 1;

  state.failedRequests += 1;

  state.totalResponseTime +=
    responseTime;

  state.averageResponseTime =
    state.totalResponseTime /
    state.requests;

  state.successRate =
    (
      state.successfulRequests /
      state.requests
    ) * 100;

  state.lastRequestAt =
    new Date();

  state.lastFailureAt =
    new Date();
}

export function getAIProviderTelemetry(
  provider: AIProviderName
): AIProviderTelemetry {

  return {
    ...getOrCreate(provider),
  };
}

export function getAllAIProviderTelemetry():
  AIProviderTelemetry[] {

  return [
    ...telemetry.values(),
  ].map(
    (item) => ({
      ...item,
    })
  );
}

import Subscription from "../../models/subscription.model.js";

import {
  AI_FEATURE_KEYS,
  AI_PLAN_LIMITS,
  type AIUsageFeature,
  type SubscriptionPlan,
} from "../../constants/ai-usage.constants.js";

/* =========================================================
   Types
========================================================= */

export interface AIQuotaState {
  feature: AIUsageFeature;

  used: number;

  limit: number;

  remaining: number;

  resetAt: Date;
}

export interface AIQuotaError extends Error {
  code: "AI_QUOTA_EXCEEDED";

  feature: AIUsageFeature;

  limit: number;

  used: number;

  resetAt: Date;
}

/* =========================================================
   Constants
========================================================= */

/**
 * Legacy plan value that may exist in older database
 * documents created by the previous admin implementation.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * Do NOT add "pro" to the Subscription schema enum.
 *
 * We normalize old documents to the canonical
 * "pro-monthly" plan before saving.
 */
const LEGACY_PRO_PLAN = "pro";

const CANONICAL_PRO_PLAN: SubscriptionPlan =
  "pro-monthly";

/* =========================================================
   Date Helpers
========================================================= */

/**
 * Calculate the next daily reset time.
 *
 * Uses the server's local calendar day.
 */
function getResetAt(): Date {
  const reset = new Date();

  reset.setHours(
    24,
    0,
    0,
    0
  );

  return reset;
}

/**
 * Check whether two dates belong to
 * the same calendar day.
 */
function isSameDay(
  first: Date,
  second: Date
): boolean {
  return (
    first.getFullYear() ===
      second.getFullYear() &&
    first.getMonth() ===
      second.getMonth() &&
    first.getDate() ===
      second.getDate()
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   Subscription
========================================================= */

/**
 * Load the user's subscription.
 */
async function getSubscription(
  userId: string
) {
  if (!userId?.trim()) {
    throw new Error(
      "Authenticated userId is required for AI quota checks."
    );
  }

  const subscription =
    await Subscription.findOne({
      user: userId,
    });

  if (!subscription) {
    throw new Error(
      "Subscription not found."
    );
  }

  return subscription;
}

/* =========================================================
   Plan Normalization
========================================================= */

/**
 * Normalize subscription plan values.
 *
 * Canonical plans:
 *
 * - free
 * - trial
 * - pro-monthly
 * - pro-yearly
 *
 * Legacy:
 *
 * - pro
 *
 * The legacy "pro" value is mapped to "pro-monthly"
 * so old documents do not break quota operations.
 *
 * Unknown values fall back to free.
 */
function normalizePlan(
  value: unknown
): SubscriptionPlan {
  switch (value) {
    case "free":
      return "free";

    case "trial":
      return "trial";

    case "pro-monthly":
      return "pro-monthly";

    case "pro-yearly":
      return "pro-yearly";

    /**
     * Backward compatibility for old admin code.
     */
    case LEGACY_PRO_PLAN:
      return CANONICAL_PRO_PLAN;

    default:
      return "free";
  }
}

/**
 * Normalize a subscription document before a save.
 *
 * This is important because old database records may contain
 * plan: "pro", which is no longer allowed by the Mongoose enum.
 */
function normalizeSubscriptionPlan(
  subscription: any
): SubscriptionPlan {
  const normalizedPlan =
    normalizePlan(
      subscription.plan
    );

  if (
    subscription.plan !==
    normalizedPlan
  ) {
    subscription.plan =
      normalizedPlan;
  }

  return normalizedPlan;
}

/* =========================================================
   Daily Usage Reset
========================================================= */

/**
 * Reset daily AI usage when the calendar day changes.
 *
 * Older subscription documents are handled safely.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * Legacy plan values are normalized BEFORE save().
 * This prevents Mongoose enum validation failures.
 */
async function resetUsageIfRequired(
  subscription: any
): Promise<void> {
  const now = new Date();

  /**
   * Normalize legacy subscription plan BEFORE
   * any possible save operation.
   */
  normalizeSubscriptionPlan(
    subscription
  );

  const lastResetValue =
    subscription.usage?.lastReset;

  const lastReset =
    lastResetValue
      ? new Date(lastResetValue)
      : undefined;

  const shouldReset =
    !lastReset ||
    !isSameDay(
      now,
      lastReset
    );

  if (!shouldReset) {
    /**
     * If the plan itself was legacy and was normalized,
     * persist that migration even when usage does not need
     * a daily reset.
     */
    if (
      subscription.isModified?.(
        "plan"
      )
    ) {
      await subscription.save();
    }

    return;
  }

  if (!subscription.usage) {
    subscription.usage = {};
  }

  subscription.usage.resumeToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.atsToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.interviewToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.coverLetterToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.linkedinToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.careerToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.recruiterToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.generalToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.totalToday = 0;

  subscription.usage.lastReset =
    now;

  await subscription.save();
}

/* =========================================================
   Usage Helpers
========================================================= */

/**
 * Read current usage for a logical AI feature.
 */
function getUsageValue(
  subscription: any,
  feature: AIUsageFeature
): number {
  const usageKey =
    AI_FEATURE_KEYS[feature];

  const value =
    subscription.usage?.[
      usageKey
    ];

  if (
    typeof value !== "number" ||
    !Number.isFinite(value) ||
    value < 0
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Math.floor(value);
}

/**
 * Set usage for a logical AI feature.
 */
function setUsageValue(
  subscription: any,
  feature: AIUsageFeature,
  value: number
): void {
  const usageKey =
    AI_FEATURE_KEYS[feature];

  if (!subscription.usage) {
    subscription.usage = {};
  }

  subscription.usage[usageKey] =
    Math.max(
      0,
      Math.floor(value)
    );
}

/* =========================================================
   Quota Error
========================================================= */

/**
 * Build a standardized quota error.
 */
function createQuotaExceededError(
  feature: AIUsageFeature,
  limit: number,
  used: number
): AIQuotaError {
  const error =
    new Error(
      "Daily AI usage limit reached."
    ) as AIQuotaError;

  error.code =
    "AI_QUOTA_EXCEEDED";

  error.feature =
    feature;

  error.limit =
    limit;

  error.used =
    used;

  error.resetAt =
    getResetAt();

  return error;
}

/* =========================================================
   Plan Limit
========================================================= */

/**
 * Get the canonical plan and its daily AI limit.
 */
function getPlanAndLimit(
  subscription: any
): {
  plan: SubscriptionPlan;
  limit: number;
} {
  const plan =
    normalizeSubscriptionPlan(
      subscription
    );

  const limit =
    AI_PLAN_LIMITS[
      plan
    ].daily;

  return {
    plan,
    limit,
  };
}

/* =========================================================
   Assert Quota
========================================================= */

/**
 * Check whether the user can consume
 * one AI request.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * This function does NOT increment usage.
 */
export async function assertAIQuota(
  userId: string,
  feature: AIUsageFeature
): Promise<AIQuotaState> {
  const subscription =
    await getSubscription(
      userId
    );

  await resetUsageIfRequired(
    subscription
  );

  const {
    limit,
  } =
    getPlanAndLimit(
      subscription
    );

  const used =
    getUsageValue(
      subscription,
      feature
    );

  const remaining =
    Math.max(
      limit - used,
      0
    );

  if (
    used >= limit
  ) {
    throw createQuotaExceededError(
      feature,
      limit,
      used
    );
  }

  return {
    feature,

    used,

    limit,

    remaining,

    resetAt:
      getResetAt(),
  };
}

/* =========================================================
   Commit Usage
========================================================= */

/**
 * Commit one successful AI usage.
 *
 * Usage is incremented ONLY after the AI operation
 * successfully resolves.
 */
export async function commitAIUsage(
  userId: string,
  feature: AIUsageFeature
): Promise<AIQuotaState> {
  const subscription =
    await getSubscription(
      userId
    );

  await resetUsageIfRequired(
    subscription
  );

  const {
    limit,
  } =
    getPlanAndLimit(
      subscription
    );

  const current =
    getUsageValue(
      subscription,
      feature
    );

  /**
   * Re-check quota immediately before
   * incrementing.
   */
  if (
    current >= limit
  ) {
    throw createQuotaExceededError(
      feature,
      limit,
      current
    );
  }

  const nextValue =
    current + 1;

  setUsageValue(
    subscription,
    feature,
    nextValue
  );

  /**
   * Keep totalToday synchronized.
   */
  const currentTotal =
    typeof subscription
      .usage
      ?.totalToday ===
    "number"
      ? Math.max(
          0,
          Math.floor(
            subscription
              .usage
              .totalToday
          )
        )
      : 0;

  subscription.usage.totalToday =
    currentTotal + 1;

  /**
   * Ensure any legacy "pro" plan is
   * normalized before save().
   */
  normalizeSubscriptionPlan(
    subscription
  );

  await subscription.save();

  return {
    feature,

    used:
      nextValue,

    limit,

    remaining:
      Math.max(
        limit -
          nextValue,
        0
      ),

    resetAt:
      getResetAt(),
  };
}

/* =========================================================
   Consume Quota
========================================================= */

/**
 * Convenience helper:
 *
 * Check quota
 *      â†“
 * Execute AI operation
 *      â†“
 * Commit successful usage
 *
 * Failed AI operations do not consume quota.
 */
export async function consumeAIQuota<T>(
  userId: string,
  feature: AIUsageFeature,
  operation: () => Promise<T>
): Promise<{
  result: T;
  usage: AIQuotaState;
}> {
  await assertAIQuota(
    userId,
    feature
  );

  const result =
    await operation();

  const usage =
    await commitAIUsage(
      userId,
      feature
    );

  return {
    result,
    usage,
  };
}
