FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\AIManager.ts

============================================================

import type {
  AIProviderAdapter,
  AIProviderName,
  AIResponse,
} from "./types/provider.types.js";

import {
  AIProviderError,
} from "./utils/AIErrors.js";

import ProviderHealth from "./ProviderHealth.js";

import {
  recordAIProviderFailure,
  recordAIProviderSuccess,
} from "./ai-telemetry.service.js";

export interface AIManagerResult
  extends AIResponse {
  attempts: number;

  attemptedProviders:
    AIProviderName[];
}

interface ProviderFailure {
  provider: AIProviderName;

  message: string;

  status?: number;
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 * AI Manager
 * ============================================================
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 *
 * - Provider availability
 * - Ordered fallback
 * - Health-aware provider selection
 * - Provider failure classification
 * - Safe telemetry
 * - No duplicated application-level retries
 *
 * NOTE:
 * Interview services already perform their own
 * higher-level retry logic. Therefore this manager
 * intentionally performs ONE request per provider
 * per run.
 * ============================================================
 */

export class AIManager {
  private readonly providers:
    AIProviderAdapter[];

  constructor(
    providers: AIProviderAdapter[]
  ) {
    if (!providers.length) {
      throw new Error(
        "AIManager requires at least one provider."
      );
    }

    this.providers = [
      ...providers,
    ];
  }

  async generate(
    prompt: string
  ): Promise<AIManagerResult> {
    if (
      !prompt ||
      !prompt.trim()
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "AI prompt cannot be empty."
      );
    }

    const attemptedProviders:
      AIProviderName[] = [];

    const errors:
      ProviderFailure[] = [];

    let configuredProviderCount =
      0;

    let lastError:
      unknown;

    /**
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * First pass:
     *
     * Try every configured provider that is currently healthy.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    for (
      const provider of
        this.providers
    ) {
      const providerName =
        provider.name;

      /**
       * Availability means configured.
       */
      let available = false;

      try {
        available =
          await provider.isAvailable();
      } catch (error) {
        lastError = error;

        console.error(
          `[AI] Availability check failed: ${providerName}`,
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : error
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (!available) {
        continue;
      }

      configuredProviderCount +=
        1;

      /**
       * Healthy providers get first priority.
       */
      if (
        !ProviderHealth.canUse(
          providerName
        )
      ) {
        console.warn(
          `[AI] Provider temporarily unhealthy: ${providerName}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      console.log(
        `[AI] Trying provider: ${providerName}`
      );

      attemptedProviders.push(
        providerName
      );

      const startedAt =
        Date.now();

      try {
        const response =
          await provider.generate(
            prompt
          );

        const latency =
          response.latency ??
          Date.now() -
            startedAt;

        if (
          !response.text?.trim()
        ) {
          throw new AIProviderError(
            `${providerName} returned an empty response.`,
            providerName,
            undefined,
            false,
            "EMPTY_RESPONSE"
          );
        }

        ProviderHealth.markSuccess(
          providerName
        );

        recordAIProviderSuccess(
          providerName,
          latency
        );

        console.log(
          `[AI] Provider success: ${providerName}`
        );

        return {
          ...response,

          attempts:
            attemptedProviders.length,

          attemptedProviders,
        };
      } catch (error) {
        lastError =
          error;

        const latency =
          Date.now() -
          startedAt;

        const providerError =
          error instanceof
          AIProviderError
            ? error
            : new AIProviderError(
                error instanceof Error
                  ? error.message
                  : `${providerName} request failed.`,
                providerName,
                undefined,
                true
              );

        ProviderHealth.markFailure(
          providerName
        );

        recordAIProviderFailure(
          providerName,
          latency
        );

        errors.push({
          provider:
            providerName,

          message:
            providerError.message,

          status:
            providerError.status,
        });

        console.error(
          `[AI] Provider failed: ${providerName}`,
          {
            message:
              providerError.message,

            status:
              providerError.status,

            latency,
          }
        );

        /**
         * Continue immediately to the next provider.
         */
        continue;
      }
    }

    /**
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * Second pass:
     *
     * If all configured providers were temporarily unhealthy,
     * do NOT incorrectly say "not configured".
     *
     * Give each configured provider one more opportunity.
     *
     * This is intentionally only ONE additional pass.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      configuredProviderCount >
        0 &&
      attemptedProviders.length ===
        0
    ) {
      console.warn(
        "[AI] All configured providers are temporarily unhealthy. Performing recovery pass."
      );

      for (
        const provider of
          this.providers
      ) {
        const providerName =
          provider.name;

        let available = false;

        try {
          available =
            await provider.isAvailable();
        } catch {
          continue;
        }

        if (!available) {
          continue;
        }

        /**
         * Don't wait for cooldown here.
         *
         * The purpose is recovery after all
         * providers entered cooldown simultaneously.
         */
        if (
          attemptedProviders.includes(
            providerName
          )
        ) {
          continue;
        }

        attemptedProviders.push(
          providerName
        );

        const startedAt =
          Date.now();

        try {
          console.log(
            `[AI] Recovery attempt: ${providerName}`
          );

          const response =
            await provider.generate(
              prompt
            );

          const latency =
            response.latency ??
            Date.now() -
              startedAt;

          if (
            !response.text?.trim()
          ) {
            throw new AIProviderError(
              `${providerName} returned an empty response.`,
              providerName,
              undefined,
              false,
              "EMPTY_RESPONSE"
            );
          }

          ProviderHealth.markSuccess(
            providerName
          );

          recordAIProviderSuccess(
            providerName,
            latency
          );

          return {
            ...response,

            attempts:
              attemptedProviders.length,

            attemptedProviders,
          };
        } catch (error) {
          lastError =
            error;

          const latency =
            Date.now() -
            startedAt;

          const providerError =
            error instanceof
            AIProviderError
              ? error
              : new AIProviderError(
                  error instanceof Error
                    ? error.message
                    : `${providerName} request failed.`,
                  providerName,
                  undefined,
                  true
                );

          ProviderHealth.markFailure(
            providerName
          );

          recordAIProviderFailure(
            providerName,
            latency
          );

          errors.push({
            provider:
              providerName,

            message:
              providerError.message,

            status:
              providerError.status,
          });

          console.error(
            `[AI] Recovery provider failed: ${providerName}`,
            {
              message:
                providerError.message,

              status:
                providerError.status,

              latency,
            }
          );
        }
      }
    }

    /**
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * No provider configured.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      configuredProviderCount ===
      0
    ) {
      throw new AIProviderError(
        "No AI provider is configured. Check OPENAI_API_KEY, GEMINI_API_KEY, and OPENROUTER_API_KEY.",
        "system",
        undefined,
        false,
        "NO_PROVIDER_CONFIGURED"
      );
    }

    /**
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * All providers failed.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.error(
      "[AI] All configured providers failed.",
      {
        attemptedProviders,
        errors,
      }
    );

    const errorSummary =
      errors.length
        ? errors
            .map(
              (item) =>
                `${item.provider}: ${item.message}`
            )
            .join(" | ")
        : "No provider request succeeded.";

    throw new AIProviderError(
      `All configured AI providers failed. ${errorSummary}`,
      errors.at(-1)
        ?.provider ??
        attemptedProviders.at(-1) ??
        "system",
      errors.at(-1)?.status,
      true,
      "ALL_PROVIDERS_FAILED"
    );
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\ProviderHealth.ts

import type {
  AIProviderName,
} from "./types/provider.types.js";

interface ProviderHealthState {
  failures: number;

  successes: number;

  totalRequests: number;

  lastFailureAt?: number;

  lastSuccessAt?: number;

  cooldownUntil?: number;
}

class ProviderHealthManager {
  private readonly states =
    new Map<
      AIProviderName,
      ProviderHealthState
    >();

  /**
   * Minimum cooldown after a provider failure.
   */
  private readonly baseCooldownMs =
    2_000;

  /**
   * Maximum cooldown.
   */
  private readonly maxCooldownMs =
    60_000;

  private getState(
    provider: AIProviderName
  ): ProviderHealthState {
    const existing =
      this.states.get(provider);

    if (existing) {
      return existing;
    }

    const state: ProviderHealthState = {
      failures: 0,
      successes: 0,
      totalRequests: 0,
    };

    this.states.set(
      provider,
      state
    );

    return state;
  }

  canUse(
    provider: AIProviderName
  ): boolean {
    const state =
      this.getState(provider);

    if (
      !state.cooldownUntil
    ) {
      return true;
    }

    if (
      Date.now() >=
      state.cooldownUntil
    ) {
      state.cooldownUntil =
        undefined;

      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }

  markSuccess(
    provider: AIProviderName
  ): void {
    const state =
      this.getState(provider);

    state.totalRequests += 1;

    state.successes += 1;

    state.lastSuccessAt =
      Date.now();

    /**
     * Successful request restores
     * provider availability.
     */
    state.failures = 0;

    state.cooldownUntil =
      undefined;
  }

  markFailure(
    provider: AIProviderName
  ): void {
    const state =
      this.getState(provider);

    state.totalRequests += 1;

    state.failures += 1;

    state.lastFailureAt =
      Date.now();

    /**
     * Exponential backoff:
     *
     * failure 1 -> 2s
     * failure 2 -> 4s
     * failure 3 -> 8s
     * ...
     *
     * Maximum 60 seconds.
     */
    const exponent =
      Math.min(
        state.failures - 1,
        5
      );

    const cooldown =
      Math.min(
        this.baseCooldownMs *
          2 ** exponent,
        this.maxCooldownMs
      );

    state.cooldownUntil =
      Date.now() +
      cooldown;
  }

  getHealth(
    provider: AIProviderName
  ) {
    const state =
      this.getState(provider);

    const successRate =
      state.totalRequests === 0
        ? 100
        :
          (
            state.successes /
            state.totalRequests
          ) *
          100;

    return {
      provider,

      totalRequests:
        state.totalRequests,

      successes:
        state.successes,

      failures:
        state.failures,

      successRate,

      healthy:
        this.canUse(provider),

      lastFailureAt:
        state.lastFailureAt,

      lastSuccessAt:
        state.lastSuccessAt,

      cooldownUntil:
        state.cooldownUntil,

      failuresSinceSuccess:
        state.failures,
    };
  }

  getAllHealth() {
    return [
      ...this.states.keys(),
    ].map(
      (provider) =>
        this.getHealth(
          provider
        )
    );
  }

  reset(
    provider?: AIProviderName
  ): void {
    if (provider) {
      this.states.delete(
        provider
      );

      return;
    }

    this.states.clear();
  }
}

export default new ProviderHealthManager();

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\providers\GeminiProvider.ts

import {
  GoogleGenAI,
} from "@google/genai";

import type {
  AIProviderAdapter,
  AIResponse,
} from "../types/provider.types.js";

import {
  AIProviderError,
  AIRateLimitError,
  AIUnavailableError,
  AIResponseError,
  normalizeAIError,
} from "../utils/AIErrors.js";

const DEFAULT_MODEL =
  "gemini-2.5-flash";

export class GeminiProvider
  implements AIProviderAdapter
{
  readonly name =
    "gemini" as const;

  private client:
    GoogleGenAI | null = null;

  private getClient(): GoogleGenAI {
    const apiKey =
      process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim();

    if (
      !apiKey ||
      apiKey.startsWith("YOUR_")
    ) {
      throw new AIProviderError(
        "Gemini API key is not configured.",
        this.name,
        401,
        false,
        "NOT_CONFIGURED"
      );
    }

    if (!this.client) {
      this.client =
        new GoogleGenAI({
          apiKey,
        });
    }

    return this.client;
  }

  async isAvailable(): Promise<boolean> {
    const key =
      process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim();

    return Boolean(
      key &&
      !key.startsWith("YOUR_")
    );
  }

  async generate(
    prompt: string
  ): Promise<AIResponse> {
    const model =
      process.env.GEMINI_MODEL?.trim() ||
      DEFAULT_MODEL;

    const startedAt =
      Date.now();

    try {
      const client =
        this.getClient();

      const response =
        await client.models.generateContent(
          {
            model,

            contents: prompt,

            config: {
              responseMimeType:
                "application/json",

              temperature: 0.2,
            },
          }
        );

      const latency =
        Date.now() - startedAt;

      const text =
        response.text;

      if (
        typeof text !== "string" ||
        !text.trim()
      ) {
        throw new AIResponseError(
          "Gemini returned an empty response.",
          this.name,
          200
        );
      }

      let json: unknown;

      try {
        json =
          JSON.parse(
            text.trim()
          );
      } catch {
        throw new AIResponseError(
          "Gemini returned invalid JSON.",
          this.name,
          200
        );
      }

      return {
        text: text.trim(),

        json,

        provider: this.name,

        model,

        latency,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof
        AIProviderError
      ) {
        throw error;
      }

      /**
       * @google/genai SDK errors can have
       * status/statusCode/code/message.
       */
      const candidate =
        error as {
          status?: number;
          statusCode?: number;
          code?: number | string;
          message?: string;
        };

      const status =
        typeof candidate.status ===
        "number"
          ? candidate.status
          : typeof candidate.statusCode ===
              "number"
            ? candidate.statusCode
            : undefined;

      const message =
        typeof candidate.message ===
        "string"
          ? candidate.message
          : "Gemini request failed.";

      /**
       * Rate limit.
       */
      if (status === 429) {
        throw new AIRateLimitError(
          "Gemini rate limit or quota limit reached.",
          this.name,
          429
        );
      }

      /**
       * Temporary Google service error.
       */
      if (
        status === 408 ||
        status === 500 ||
        status === 502 ||
        status === 503 ||
        status === 504
      ) {
        throw new AIUnavailableError(
          `Gemini service is temporarily unavailable: ${message}`,
          this.name,
          status
        );
      }

      /**
       * Authentication.
       */
      if (
        status === 401 ||
        status === 403
      ) {
        throw new AIProviderError(
          "Gemini authentication failed. Check GEMINI_API_KEY.",
          this.name,
          status,
          false,
          "AUTHENTICATION_FAILED"
        );
      }

      /**
       * Invalid model/request.
       */
      if (
        status === 400 ||
        status === 404
      ) {
        throw new AIProviderError(
          `Gemini rejected the request: ${message}`,
          this.name,
          status,
          false,
          "BAD_REQUEST"
        );
      }

      /**
       * Final safe normalization.
       */
      throw normalizeAIError(
        error,
        this.name
      );
    }
  }
}

import type {
  AIProviderAdapter,
  AIResponse,
} from "../types/provider.types.js";

import {
  aiHttp,
} from "../utils/AIHttpClient.js";

import {
  AIProviderError,
  AIRateLimitError,
  AITimeoutError,
  AIUnavailableError,
  AIResponseError,
  normalizeAIError,
} from "../utils/AIErrors.js";

const DEFAULT_MODEL =
  "gpt-5-mini";

export class OpenAIProvider
  implements AIProviderAdapter
{
  readonly name =
    "openai" as const;

  async isAvailable(): Promise<boolean> {
    const key =
      process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY?.trim();

    return Boolean(
      key &&
      !key.startsWith("YOUR_")
    );
  }

  async generate(
    prompt: string
  ): Promise<AIResponse> {
    const apiKey =
      process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY?.trim();

    if (
      !apiKey ||
      apiKey.startsWith("YOUR_")
    ) {
      throw new AIProviderError(
        "OpenAI API key is not configured.",
        this.name,
        401,
        false,
        "NOT_CONFIGURED"
      );
    }

    const model =
      process.env.OPENAI_MODEL?.trim() ||
      DEFAULT_MODEL;

    const startedAt =
      Date.now();

    try {
      const response =
        await aiHttp.post(
          "https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions",
          {
            model,

            messages: [
              {
                role: "system",
                content:
                  "You are CareerBoost AI. Return valid JSON only. Never use markdown fences.",
              },
              {
                role: "user",
                content: prompt,
              },
            ],

            response_format: {
              type: "json_object",
            },
          },
          {
            headers: {
              Authorization:
                `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
            },
          }
        );

      const latency =
        Date.now() - startedAt;

      /**
       * HTTP error handling.
       */
      if (
        response.status === 429
      ) {
        throw new AIRateLimitError(
          "OpenAI rate limit or quota limit reached.",
          this.name,
          429
        );
      }

      if (
        response.status === 408 ||
        response.status === 500 ||
        response.status === 502 ||
        response.status === 503 ||
        response.status === 504
      ) {
        throw new AIUnavailableError(
          "OpenAI service is temporarily unavailable.",
          this.name,
          response.status
        );
      }

      if (
        response.status === 401 ||
        response.status === 403
      ) {
        throw new AIProviderError(
          "OpenAI authentication failed. Check OPENAI_API_KEY.",
          this.name,
          response.status,
          false,
          "AUTHENTICATION_FAILED"
        );
      }

      if (
        response.status < 200 ||
        response.status >= 300
      ) {
        throw new AIProviderError(
          `OpenAI rejected the request with status ${response.status}.`,
          this.name,
          response.status,
          false,
          "HTTP_ERROR"
        );
      }

      const text =
        response.data
          ?.choices?.[0]
          ?.message?.content;

      if (
        typeof text !== "string" ||
        !text.trim()
      ) {
        throw new AIResponseError(
          "OpenAI returned an empty response.",
          this.name,
          response.status
        );
      }

      let json: unknown;

      try {
        json =
          JSON.parse(
            text.trim()
          );
      } catch {
        throw new AIResponseError(
          "OpenAI returned invalid JSON.",
          this.name,
          response.status
        );
      }

      return {
        text: text.trim(),

        json,

        provider: this.name,

        model,

        latency,

        tokensUsed:
          response.data
            ?.usage
            ?.total_tokens,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof
        AIProviderError
      ) {
        throw error;
      }

      const normalized =
        normalizeAIError(
          error,
          this.name
        );

      /**
       * Preserve timeout semantics.
       */
      if (
        normalized instanceof
        AITimeoutError
      ) {
        throw normalized;
      }

      throw normalized;
    }
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\providers\OpenRouterProvider.ts

============================================================

import type {
  AIProviderAdapter,
  AIResponse,
} from "../types/provider.types.js";

import {
  aiHttp,
} from "../utils/AIHttpClient.js";

import {
  AIProviderError,
  AIRateLimitError,
  AIUnavailableError,
  AIResponseError,
  normalizeAIError,
} from "../utils/AIErrors.js";

const DEFAULT_MODEL =
  "openrouter/free";

export class OpenRouterProvider
  implements AIProviderAdapter
{
  readonly name =
    "openrouter" as const;

  async isAvailable(): Promise<boolean> {
    const key =
      process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY?.trim();

    return Boolean(
      key &&
      !key.startsWith("YOUR_")
    );
  }

  async generate(
    prompt: string
  ): Promise<AIResponse> {
    const apiKey =
      process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY?.trim();

    if (
      !apiKey ||
      apiKey.startsWith("YOUR_")
    ) {
      throw new AIProviderError(
        "OpenRouter API key is not configured.",
        this.name,
        401,
        false,
        "NOT_CONFIGURED"
      );
    }

    const model =
      process.env.OPENROUTER_MODEL?.trim() ||
      DEFAULT_MODEL;

    const startedAt =
      Date.now();

    try {
      const response =
        await aiHttp.post(
          "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",
          {
            model,

            messages: [
              {
                role: "system",
                content:
                  "You are CareerBoost AI. Return valid JSON only. Never use markdown fences.",
              },
              {
                role: "user",
                content: prompt,
              },
            ],

            /**
             * Ask OpenRouter-compatible models
             * for JSON where supported.
             *
             * The parser below still validates
             * the actual response.
             */
            response_format: {
              type: "json_object",
            },

            stream: false,
          },
          {
            headers: {
              Authorization:
                `Bearer ${apiKey}`,

              "HTTP-Referer":
                process.env.APP_URL ||
                "http://localhost:5173",

              "X-Title":
                "CareerBoost AI",
            },
          }
        );

      const latency =
        Date.now() - startedAt;

      /**
       * HTTP status handling.
       */
      if (
        response.status === 429
      ) {
        throw new AIRateLimitError(
          "OpenRouter rate limit or quota reached.",
          this.name,
          429
        );
      }

      if (
        response.status === 401 ||
        response.status === 403
      ) {
        throw new AIProviderError(
          "OpenRouter authentication failed. Check OPENROUTER_API_KEY.",
          this.name,
          response.status,
          false,
          "AUTHENTICATION_FAILED"
        );
      }

      if (
        response.status === 408 ||
        response.status === 500 ||
        response.status === 502 ||
        response.status === 503 ||
        response.status === 504
      ) {
        throw new AIUnavailableError(
          "OpenRouter service is temporarily unavailable.",
          this.name,
          response.status
        );
      }

      if (
        response.status < 200 ||
        response.status >= 300
      ) {
        throw new AIProviderError(
          `OpenRouter rejected the request with status ${response.status}.`,
          this.name,
          response.status,
          false,
          "HTTP_ERROR"
        );
      }

      /**
       * OpenRouter may return content as:
       *
       * message.content
       *
       * Some providers/models can return
       * content arrays, so normalize both.
       */
      const rawContent =
        response.data
          ?.choices?.[0]
          ?.message
          ?.content;

      let text: string | null =
        null;

      if (
        typeof rawContent === "string"
      ) {
        text =
          rawContent.trim();
      } else if (
        Array.isArray(rawContent)
      ) {
        const parts =
          rawContent
            .map((part: unknown) => {
              if (
                typeof part === "string"
              ) {
                return part;
              }

              if (
                part &&
                typeof part ===
                  "object" &&
                "text" in part
              ) {
                const value =
                  (
                    part as {
                      text?: unknown;
                    }
                  ).text;

                return typeof value ===
                  "string"
                  ? value
                  : "";
              }

              return "";
            })
            .filter(Boolean);

        if (parts.length) {
          text =
            parts.join("\n").trim();
        }
      }

      /**
       * Some OpenRouter responses can contain
       * a refusal/message field.
       */
      if (
        (!text || !text.trim()) &&
        typeof response.data
          ?.choices?.[0]
          ?.message
          ?.refusal === "string"
      ) {
        throw new AIResponseError(
          `OpenRouter model refused the request: ${response.data.choices[0].message.refusal}`,
          this.name,
          response.status
        );
      }

      if (
        !text ||
        !text.trim()
      ) {
        /**
         * Log only safe structural information.
         *
         * Never log the API key.
         */
        console.error(
          "[AI][OpenRouter] Empty model response.",
          {
            status:
              response.status,

            model,

            finishReason:
              response.data
                ?.choices?.[0]
                ?.finish_reason,

            id:
              response.data?.id,
          }
        );

        throw new AIResponseError(
          "OpenRouter returned an empty response.",
          this.name,
          response.status
        );
      }

      /**
       * Remove accidental markdown fences
       * before JSON parsing.
       */
      const cleanedText =
        text
          .replace(
            /^```json\s*/i,
            ""
          )
          .replace(
            /^```\s*/i,
            ""
          )
          .replace(
            /\s*```$/i,
            ""
          )
          .trim();

      let json: unknown;

      try {
        json =
          JSON.parse(
            cleanedText
          );
      } catch {
        throw new AIResponseError(
          "OpenRouter returned invalid JSON.",
          this.name,
          response.status
        );
      }

      return {
        text: cleanedText,

        json,

        provider: this.name,

        model,

        latency,

        tokensUsed:
          response.data
            ?.usage
            ?.total_tokens,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof
        AIProviderError
      ) {
        throw error;
      }

      throw normalizeAIError(
        error,
        this.name
      );
    }
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\utils\ai-cache.ts

============================================================

interface CacheEntry<T> {
  value: T;
  expiresAt: number;
}

const cache =
  new Map<string, CacheEntry<unknown>>();

export function getAIFromCache<T>(
  key: string
): T | undefined {
  const entry = cache.get(key);

  if (!entry) {
    return undefined;
  }

  if (
    Date.now() >=
    entry.expiresAt
  ) {
    cache.delete(key);
    return undefined;
  }

  return entry.value as T;
}

export function setAIInCache<T>(
  key: string,
  value: T,
  ttlSeconds = 30
): void {
  cache.set(key, {
    value,
    expiresAt:
      Date.now() +
      ttlSeconds * 1_000,
  });
}

export function deleteAIFromCache(
  key: string
): void {
  cache.delete(key);
}

export function clearAICache(): void {
  cache.clear();
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\utils\ai-json.ts

============================================================

export function parseAIJson<T = unknown>(
  content: string
): T {
  if (
    typeof content !== "string" ||
    !content.trim()
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "AI response is empty."
    );
  }

  const cleaned =
    content
      .trim()
      .replace(
        /^```(?:json)?\s*/i,
        ""
      )
      .replace(
        /\s*```$/i,
        ""
      )
      .trim();

  try {
    return JSON.parse(cleaned) as T;
  } catch {
    const objectMatch =
      cleaned.match(
        /\{[\s\S]*\}/
      );

    const arrayMatch =
      cleaned.match(
        /\[[\s\S]*\]/
      );

    const candidate =
      objectMatch?.[0] ??
      arrayMatch?.[0];

    if (!candidate) {
      throw new Error(
        "AI did not return valid JSON."
      );
    }

    try {
      return JSON.parse(candidate) as T;
    } catch {
      throw new Error(
        "Unable to parse AI JSON response."
      );
    }
  }
}

  latency: number;
}

export function logAIRequest(
  input: AILoggerInput
): void {
  /*
   * Never log:
   * - prompts
   * - API keys
   * - resume content
   * - AI response content
   * - authentication tokens
   */

  console.info(
    "[AI]",
    {
      module:
        input.module,

      provider:
        input.provider,

      success:
        input.success,

      retries:
        input.retries ?? 0,

      latency:
        input.latency,

      timestamp:
        new Date().toISOString(),
    }
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\utils\ai-retry.ts

============================================================

export interface AIRetryOptions {
  retries?: number;
  initialDelay?: number;
  maxDelay?: number;
  backoffMultiplier?: number;
}

const sleep = (
  ms: number
): Promise<void> =>
  new Promise((resolve) =>
    setTimeout(resolve, ms)
  );

export async function retryAI<T>(
  operation: () => Promise<T>,
  options: AIRetryOptions = {}
): Promise<T> {
  const {
    retries = 2,
    initialDelay = 500,
    maxDelay = 5_000,
    backoffMultiplier = 2,
  } = options;

  let lastError: unknown;

  for (
    let attempt = 0;
    attempt <= retries;
    attempt++
  ) {
    try {
      return await operation();
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;

      if (attempt === retries) {
        break;
      }

      const delay = Math.min(
        initialDelay *
          Math.pow(
            backoffMultiplier,
            attempt
          ),
        maxDelay
      );

      await sleep(delay);
    }
  }

  throw (
    lastError instanceof Error
      ? lastError
      : new Error(
          "AI operation failed after retries."
        )
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\utils\ai-validator.ts

============================================================

export type AIFieldType =
  | "string"
  | "number"
  | "boolean"
  | "array"
  | "object";

export interface AIFieldRule {
  field: string;
  type: AIFieldType;
  required?: boolean;
}

function matchesType(
  value: unknown,
  type: AIFieldType
): boolean {
  switch (type) {
    case "string":
      return typeof value === "string";

    case "number":
      return (
        typeof value === "number" &&
        Number.isFinite(value)
      );

    case "boolean":
      return typeof value === "boolean";

    case "array":
      return Array.isArray(value);

    case "object":
      return (
        typeof value === "object" &&
        value !== null &&
        !Array.isArray(value)
      );

    default:
      return false;
  }
}

export function validateAIResponse(
  value: unknown,
  rules: AIFieldRule[]
): void {
  if (
    typeof value !== "object" ||
    value === null ||
    Array.isArray(value)
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "AI response must be a JSON object."
    );
  }

  const object =
    value as Record<string, unknown>;

  for (const rule of rules) {
    const fieldValue =
      object[rule.field];

    if (
      fieldValue === undefined ||
      fieldValue === null
    ) {
      if (rule.required) {
        throw new Error(
          `AI response is missing required field: ${rule.field}`
        );
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (
      !matchesType(
        fieldValue,
        rule.type
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `AI response field "${rule.field}" must be ${rule.type}.`
      );
    }
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\utils\AIErrors.ts

============================================================

/**
 * ============================================================
 * CareerBoost AI - Provider Errors
 * ============================================================
 *
 * Centralized error types for all AI providers.
 *
 * Goals:
 * - Preserve HTTP status codes
 * - Distinguish retryable/non-retryable failures
 * - Preserve original provider error message
 * - Allow AIManager to make intelligent fallback decisions
 * - Never expose API keys/secrets
 * ============================================================
 */

export class AIProviderError extends Error {
  public readonly provider: string;
  public readonly status?: number;
  public readonly retryable: boolean;
  public readonly code?: string;

  constructor(
    message: string,
    provider: string,
    status?: number,
    retryable = false,
    code?: string
  ) {
    super(message);

    this.name = "AIProviderError";

    this.provider = provider;
    this.status = status;
    this.retryable = retryable;
    this.code = code;

    Object.setPrototypeOf(
      this,
      new.target.prototype
    );
  }
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 * Rate Limit / Quota
 * ============================================================
 */

export class AIRateLimitError
  extends AIProviderError
{
  constructor(
    message: string,
    provider: string,
    status = 429
  ) {
    super(
      message,
      provider,
      status,
      true,
      "RATE_LIMITED"
    );

    this.name = "AIRateLimitError";
  }
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 * Timeout
 * ============================================================
 */

export class AITimeoutError
  extends AIProviderError
{
  constructor(
    message: string,
    provider: string
  ) {
    super(
      message,
      provider,
      408,
      true,
      "TIMEOUT"
    );

    this.name = "AITimeoutError";
  }
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 * Temporary Provider Failure
 * ============================================================
 */

export class AIUnavailableError
  extends AIProviderError
{
  constructor(
    message: string,
    provider: string,
    status?: number
  ) {
    super(
      message,
      provider,
      status,
      true,
      "UNAVAILABLE"
    );

    this.name = "AIUnavailableError";
  }
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 * Invalid Provider Response
 * ============================================================
 */

export class AIResponseError
  extends AIProviderError
{
  constructor(
    message: string,
    provider: string,
    status?: number
  ) {
    super(
      message,
      provider,
      status,
      false,
      "INVALID_RESPONSE"
    );

    this.name = "AIResponseError";
  }
}

/**
 * ============================================================
 * Provider Error Normalizer
 * ============================================================
 *
 * Converts unknown SDK/Axios errors into safe
 * AIProviderError instances.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * Never include request headers or API keys
 * in the returned error message.
 */

export function normalizeAIError(
  error: unknown,
  provider: string
): AIProviderError {
  if (
    error instanceof AIProviderError
  ) {
    return error;
  }

  const candidate =
    error as {
      response?: {
        status?: number;
        data?: unknown;
      };

      code?: string;

      message?: string;
    };

  const status =
    candidate?.response?.status;

  const code =
    candidate?.code;

  const message =
    typeof candidate?.message ===
    "string"
      ? candidate.message
      : "Unknown AI provider error.";

  /**
   * Axios timeout.
   */
  if (
    code === "ECONNABORTED" ||
    code === "ETIMEDOUT" ||
    code === "ECONNRESET"
  ) {
    return new AITimeoutError(
      `${provider} request timed out.`,
      provider
    );
  }

  /**
   * HTTP rate limit.
   */
  if (status === 429) {
    return new AIRateLimitError(
      `${provider} rate limit or quota limit reached.`,
      provider,
      429
    );
  }

  /**
   * Temporary server/provider errors.
   */
  if (
    status === 408 ||
    status === 409 ||
    status === 425 ||
    status === 500 ||
    status === 502 ||
    status === 503 ||
    status === 504
  ) {
    return new AIUnavailableError(
      `${provider} service is temporarily unavailable.`,
      provider,
      status
    );
  }

  /**
   * Authentication errors should NOT
   * endlessly retry.
   */
  if (
    status === 401 ||
    status === 403
  ) {
    return new AIProviderError(
      `${provider} authentication failed. Check the provider API key.`,
      provider,
      status,
      false,
      "AUTHENTICATION_FAILED"
    );
  }

  /**
   * Invalid request / model.
   */
  if (
    status === 400 ||
    status === 404
  ) {
    return new AIProviderError(
      `${provider} rejected the request.`,
      provider,
      status,
      false,
      "BAD_REQUEST"
    );
  }

  /**
   * Safe fallback.
   *
   * Keep original message because provider SDK
   * errors can contain useful diagnostic details.
   */
  return new AIProviderError(
    `${provider} request failed: ${message}`,
    provider,
    status,
    false,
    "PROVIDER_ERROR"
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\lib\ai\utils\AIHttpClient.ts

============================================================

import axios from "axios";

/**
 * ============================================================
 * CareerBoost AI - Shared HTTP Client
 * ============================================================
 *
 * One centralized HTTP client for external AI providers.
 *
 * Timeout:
 * - Configurable through AI_TIMEOUT_MS
 * - Defaults to 25 seconds
 *
 * We intentionally do NOT automatically retry here.
 * Provider fallback/retry belongs to AIManager.
 * ============================================================
 */

const configuredTimeout =
  Number(
    process.env.AI_TIMEOUT_MS
  );

const timeout =
  Number.isFinite(configuredTimeout) &&
  configuredTimeout >= 5_000 &&
  configuredTimeout <= 120_000
    ? configuredTimeout
    : 25_000;

export const aiHttp =
  axios.create({
    timeout,

    headers: {
      "Content-Type":
        "application/json",

      Accept:
        "application/json",

      "HTTP-Referer":
        process.env.APP_URL ||
        "http://localhost:5173",

      "X-Title":
        "CareerBoost AI",
    },

    /**
     * Prevent Axios from throwing away
     * useful non-2xx responses before our
     * provider adapters inspect them.
     */
    validateStatus: () => true,
  });

import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { AuthRequest } from "./auth.middleware.js";
import User from "../models/user.model.js";
import AdminUsage, { IAdminUsage } from "../models/admin-usage.model.js";

export const requireAdminRole = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    if (!req.user) {
      res.status(401).json({ success: false, message: "Authentication required" });
      return;
    }

    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id).select("role").lean();
    
    if (!user || user.role !== "admin") {
      res.status(403).json({ success: false, message: "Admin role required" });
      return;
    }

    next();
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Internal server error" });
  }
};

export const logAdminAction = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  action: IAdminUsage["action"],
  target: string,
  details: string
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    if (!req.user) return;
    
    await AdminUsage.create({
      adminId: req.user.id,
      action,
      target,
      details,
      ip: req.ip || "unknown"
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to log admin action:", error);
  }
};

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\middleware\auth.middleware.ts

============================================================

import type {
  Request,
  Response,
  NextFunction,
} from "express";

import jwt, {
  type JwtPayload,
} from "jsonwebtoken";

import {
  isValidObjectId,
} from "mongoose";

import User, {
  type UserRole,
} from "../models/user.model.js";

/* ==========================================================
   JWT Payload
========================================================== */

interface AccessTokenPayload
  extends JwtPayload {
  userId: string;
}

/* ==========================================================
   Auth Request
========================================================== */

export interface AuthRequest
  extends Request {
  user?: {
    id: string;

    role: UserRole;
  };
}

/* ==========================================================
   Auth Middleware
========================================================== */

export async function authMiddleware(
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    /* ======================================================
       Read Authorization Header
    ====================================================== */

    const authHeader =
      req.headers.authorization;

    if (
      typeof authHeader !==
        "string" ||
      !authHeader.startsWith(
        "Bearer "
      )
    ) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Authorization token missing.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Extract Token
    ====================================================== */

    const token =
      authHeader
        .slice(7)
        .trim();

    if (!token) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Authorization token missing.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Access Secret
    ====================================================== */

    const accessSecret =
      process.env.JWT_ACCESS_SECRET;

    if (
      typeof accessSecret !==
        "string" ||
      accessSecret.trim().length < 32
    ) {
      console.error(
        "JWT_ACCESS_SECRET is missing or too weak."
      );

      res.status(500).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Authentication service is not configured.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Verify JWT
    ====================================================== */

    const decoded =
      jwt.verify(
        token,
        accessSecret.trim()
      ) as AccessTokenPayload;

    /* ======================================================
       Validate User ID
    ====================================================== */

    if (
      typeof decoded.userId !==
        "string" ||
      decoded.userId.trim().length ===
        0
    ) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Invalid authentication token.",
      });

      return;
    }

    const userId =
      decoded.userId.trim();

    /* ======================================================
       Validate MongoDB ObjectId
    ====================================================== */

    if (
      !isValidObjectId(
        userId
      )
    ) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Invalid authentication token.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Find User
    ====================================================== */

    const user =
      await User.findById(
        userId
      )
        .select(
          "_id role"
        )
        .lean();

    if (!user) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "User not found.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Attach Authenticated User
    ====================================================== */

    req.user = {
      id: String(
        user._id
      ),

      role:
        user.role,
    };

    /* ======================================================
       Continue
    ====================================================== */

    next();
  } catch (error) {
    /* ======================================================
       Expired Token
    ====================================================== */

    if (
      error instanceof
      jwt.TokenExpiredError
    ) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Authentication token expired.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Invalid JWT
    ====================================================== */

    if (
      error instanceof
      jwt.JsonWebTokenError
    ) {
      res.status(401).json({
        success: false,

        message:
          "Invalid authentication token.",
      });

      return;
    }

    /* ======================================================
       Unknown Error
    ====================================================== */

    console.error(
      "Authentication middleware error:",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,

      message:
        "Authentication service error.",
    });
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\middleware\error.middleware.ts

============================================================

import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';

// Middleware to handle 404 Not Found errors
const notFound = (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  const error = new Error(`Not Found - ${req.originalUrl}`);
  res.status(404);
  next(error); // Pass the error to the next error handling middleware
};

// Centralized error handling middleware
const errorHandler = (err: Error, req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  // Check if status code is already set, otherwise default to 500
  const statusCode = res.statusCode === 200 ? 500 : res.statusCode;
  res.status(statusCode);

  res.json({
    message: err.message,
    // Stack trace sirf development environment mein dikhao for debugging
    stack: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? 'ðŸ¥ž' : err.stack,
  });
};

export { notFound, errorHandler };

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\middleware\upload.middleware.ts

============================================================

import multer from "multer";
import path from "node:path";
import fs from "node:fs";

const uploadDirectory =
  path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "uploads",
    "resumes"
  );

if (
  !fs.existsSync(uploadDirectory)
) {
  fs.mkdirSync(
    uploadDirectory,
    {
      recursive: true,
    }
  );
}

const storage =
  multer.diskStorage({
    destination: (
      _req,
      _file,
      cb
    ) => {
      cb(
        null,
        uploadDirectory
      );
    },

    filename: (
      _req,
      file,
      cb
    ) => {
      const extension =
        path.extname(
          file.originalname
        ).toLowerCase();

      const baseName =
        path
          .basename(
            file.originalname,
            extension
          )
          .replace(
            /[^a-zA-Z0-9-_]/g,
            "-"
          )
          .replace(
            /-+/g,
            "-"
          )
          .slice(0, 80);

      const uniqueSuffix =
        `${Date.now()}-${Math.round(
          Math.random() * 1_000_000
        )}`;

      cb(
        null,
        `${baseName}-${uniqueSuffix}${extension}`
      );
    },
  });

const allowedMimeTypes =
  new Set([
    "application/pdf",

    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",

    "application/msword",
  ]);

const allowedExtensions =
  new Set([
    ".pdf",
    ".doc",
    ".docx",
  ]);

const fileFilter:
  multer.Options["fileFilter"] =
  (
    _req,
    file,
    cb
  ) => {
    const extension =
      path
        .extname(
          file.originalname
        )
        .toLowerCase();

    const mimeTypeAllowed =
      allowedMimeTypes.has(
        file.mimetype
      );

    const extensionAllowed =
      allowedExtensions.has(
        extension
      );

    if (
      !mimeTypeAllowed ||
      !extensionAllowed
    ) {
      return cb(
        new Error(
          "Only PDF, DOC, and DOCX resume files are allowed."
        )
      );
    }

    return cb(
      null,
      true
    );
  };

export const upload =
  multer({
    storage,

    fileFilter,

    limits: {
      fileSize:
        5 * 1024 * 1024,

      files: 1,
    },
  });

export async function getOrCreateAdminSettings(): Promise<IAdminSettings> {
  let settings = await AdminSettings.findOne();
  if (!settings) {
    settings = await AdminSettings.create({});
  }
  return settings;
}

import mongoose, {
  Schema,
  type Document,
  type Model,
} from "mongoose";

/* ==========================================================
   Enums
========================================================== */

export const JOB_STATUSES = [
  "wishlist",
  "applied",
  "screening",
  "interview",
  "technical",
  "hr",
  "offer",
  "accepted",
  "rejected",
] as const;

export type JobStatus =
  (typeof JOB_STATUSES)[number];

export const EMPLOYMENT_TYPES = [
  "full-time",
  "part-time",
  "internship",
  "contract",
  "freelance",
] as const;

export type EmploymentType =
  (typeof EMPLOYMENT_TYPES)[number];

export const WORK_MODES = [
  "remote",
  "hybrid",
  "onsite",
] as const;

export type WorkMode =
  (typeof WORK_MODES)[number];

export const JOB_PRIORITIES = [
  "low",
  "medium",
  "high",
] as const;

export type JobPriority =
  (typeof JOB_PRIORITIES)[number];

/* ==========================================================
   Nested Types
========================================================== */

export interface ISalaryRange {
  min: number;
  max: number;
  currency: string;
}

export interface IInterviewSchedule {
  date: string;
  time: string;
  interviewer?: string;
  meetingLink?: string;
  notes?: string;
}

export interface IJobTimelineItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: JobStatus;
  createdAt: string;
}

/* ==========================================================
   Document
========================================================== */

export interface IJobApplication
  extends Document {
  user: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
  company: string;
  position: string;
  location: string;
  employmentType: EmploymentType;
  workMode: WorkMode;
  priority: JobPriority;
  status: JobStatus;
  salary?: ISalaryRange;
  appliedDate: string;
  deadline?: string;
  jobUrl?: string;
  description?: string;
  notes?: string;
  interview?: IInterviewSchedule;
  timeline: IJobTimelineItem[];
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

/* ==========================================================
   Schema
========================================================== */

const salarySchema = new Schema<ISalaryRange>(
  {
    min: { type: Number, required: true },
    max: { type: Number, required: true },
    currency: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      default: "INR",
    },
  },
  { _id: false }
);

const interviewSchema =
  new Schema<IInterviewSchedule>(
    {
      date: { type: String, required: true },
      time: { type: String, required: true },
      interviewer: { type: String },
      meetingLink: { type: String },
      notes: { type: String },
    },
    { _id: false }
  );

const timelineSchema =
  new Schema<IJobTimelineItem>(
    {
      id: { type: String, required: true },
      title: { type: String, required: true },
      description: {
        type: String,
        default: "",
      },
      status: {
        type: String,
        enum: JOB_STATUSES,
        required: true,
      },
      createdAt: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
      },
    },
    { _id: false }
  );

const jobApplicationSchema =
  new Schema<IJobApplication>(
    {
      user: {
        type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: "User",
        required: true,
        index: true,
      },

      company: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        trim: true,
      },

      position: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        trim: true,
      },

      location: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        trim: true,
      },

      employmentType: {
        type: String,
        enum: EMPLOYMENT_TYPES,
        required: true,
        default: "full-time",
      },

      workMode: {
        type: String,
        enum: WORK_MODES,
        required: true,
        default: "remote",
      },

      priority: {
        type: String,
        enum: JOB_PRIORITIES,
        required: true,
        default: "medium",
      },

      status: {
        type: String,
        enum: JOB_STATUSES,
        required: true,
        default: "wishlist",
      },

      salary: {
        type: salarySchema,
      },

      appliedDate: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        default: () =>
          new Date().toISOString(),
      },

      deadline: {
        type: String,
      },

      jobUrl: {
        type: String,
      },

      description: {
        type: String,
      },

      notes: {
        type: String,
      },

      interview: {
        type: interviewSchema,
      },

      timeline: {
        type: [timelineSchema],
        default: [],
      },
    },
    {
      timestamps: true,
    }
  );

jobApplicationSchema.index({
  user: 1,
  createdAt: -1,
});

const JobApplication =
  (mongoose.models.JobApplication as
    | Model<IJobApplication>
    | undefined) ??
  mongoose.model<IJobApplication>(
    "JobApplication",
    jobApplicationSchema
  );

export default JobApplication;

import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from "mongoose";

export interface IResume extends Document {
  user: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

  title: string;

  template: string;

  personal: {
    firstName: string;
    lastName: string;
    email: string;
    phone: string;
    location: string;
    portfolio?: string;
    linkedin?: string;
    github?: string;
    website?: string;
    summary: string;
  };

  education: {
    institution: string;
    degree: string;
    field: string;
    startDate: string;
    endDate: string;
    grade?: string;
    description?: string;
  }[];

  experience: {
    company: string;
    position: string;
    location?: string;
    startDate: string;
    endDate: string;
    current: boolean;
    description: string[];
  }[];

  projects: {
    title: string;
    technologies: string[];
    description: string[];
    github?: string;
    live?: string;
  }[];

  skills: {
    technical: string[];
    soft: string[];
    tools: string[];
    languages: string[];
  };

  certifications: {
    name: string;
    issuer: string;
    issueDate?: string;
    credentialId?: string;
  }[];

  achievements: string[];

  interests: string[];

  references: {
    name: string;
    designation: string;
    company: string;
    email: string;
    phone: string;
  }[];

  atsScore: number;

  resumeScore: number;

  recruiterScore: number;

  aiImproved: boolean;

  version: number;

  isDeleted: boolean;

  createdAt: Date;

  updatedAt: Date;
}

const ResumeSchema = new Schema<IResume>(
  {
    user: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      required: true,
      index: true,
    },

    title: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      default: "My Resume",
    },

    template: {
      type: String,
      default: "ats",
    },

    personal: {
      firstName: String,
      lastName: String,
      email: String,
      phone: String,
      location: String,
      portfolio: String,
      linkedin: String,
      github: String,
      website: String,
      summary: String,
    },

    education: [
      {
        institution: String,
        degree: String,
        field: String,
        startDate: String,
        endDate: String,
        grade: String,
        description: String,
      },
    ],

    experience: [
      {
        company: String,
        position: String,
        location: String,
        startDate: String,
        endDate: String,
        current: Boolean,
        description: [String],
      },
    ],

    projects: [
      {
        title: String,
        technologies: [String],
        description: [String],
        github: String,
        live: String,
      },
    ],

    skills: {
      technical: [String],
      soft: [String],
      tools: [String],
      languages: [String],
    },

    certifications: [
      {
        name: String,
        issuer: String,
        issueDate: String,
        credentialId: String,
      },
    ],

    achievements: [String],

    interests: [String],

    references: [
      {
        name: String,
        designation: String,
        company: String,
        email: String,
        phone: String,
      },
    ],

    atsScore: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
    },

    resumeScore: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
    },

    recruiterScore: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
    },

    aiImproved: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
    },

    version: {
      type: Number,
      default: 1,
    },

    isDeleted: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
  }
);

ResumeSchema.index({
  user: 1,
  updatedAt: -1,
});

  mongoose.models.Resume ||

      type: Boolean,
      default: true,

      type: Boolean,
      default: false,

      ],
      default: "public",
    },
  },

    timestamps: true,
  }
);

          "trial",
          "pro-monthly",
          "pro-yearly",

        type: String,

        enum: [
          "active",
          "expired",
          "cancelled",
        ],

        default: "active",
      },

        type: Date,

        default: Date.now,
      },

      trialEnd: {
        type: Date,

        default: () =>
          new Date(
            Date.now() +
              3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
          ),
      },

      expiresAt: {
        type: Date,
      },

      paymentMethod: {
        type: String,
      },

      amountPaid: {
        type: Number,

        default: 0,
      },

      currency: {
        type: String,

        default: "INR",
      },

        type: String,
      },

      razorpayPaymentId: {
        type: String,
      },

      razorpaySignature: {
        type: String,
      },

      paymentDate: {
        type: Date,
      },

      usage: {
        type: usageSchema,

        default: () => ({}),
      },
    },

    {
      timestamps: true,
    }
  );

const Subscription =
  mongoose.models.Subscription ||
  mongoose.model(
    "Subscription",

  );

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\models\user.model.ts

============================================================

import mongoose, {
  Schema,
  type Model,
} from "mongoose";

import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";

/* ==========================================================
   User Roles
========================================================== */

export type UserRole =
  | "user"
  | "admin";

/* ==========================================================
   IUser Interface
========================================================== */

export interface IUser {
  name: string;

  email: string;

  password?: string;

  isProUser: boolean;

  role: UserRole;

  refreshToken?: string;

  subscription?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

  bio?: string;

  title?: string;

  location?: string;

  phone?: string;

  github?: string;

  linkedin?: string;

  portfolio?: string;

  avatar?: string;

  comparePassword(
    enteredPassword: string
  ): Promise<boolean>;
}

/* ==========================================================
   User Document
========================================================== */

export type UserDocument =
  mongoose.HydratedDocument<IUser>;

/* ==========================================================
   User Schema
========================================================== */

const userSchema =
  new Schema<IUser>(
    {
      /* ====================================================
         Basic Identity
      ==================================================== */

      name: {
        type: String,

        required: [
          true,
          "Please add a name",
        ],

        trim: true,

        minlength: [
          2,
          "Name must be at least 2 characters",
        ],

        maxlength: [
          100,
          "Name cannot exceed 100 characters",
        ],
      },

      email: {
        type: String,

        required: [
          true,
          "Please add an email",
        ],

        unique: true,

        lowercase: true,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: 254,

        match: [
          /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/,

          "Please enter a valid email",
        ],
      },

      /* ====================================================
         Authentication
      ==================================================== */

      password: {
        type: String,

        required: [
          true,
          "Please add a password",
        ],

        minlength: [
          6,
          "Password must be at least 6 characters",
        ],

        select: false,
      },

      /* ====================================================
         Account
      ==================================================== */

      isProUser: {
        type: Boolean,

        default: false,

        required: true,
      },

      role: {
        type: String,

        enum: [
          "user",
          "admin",
        ],

        default: "user",

        required: true,
      },

      /* ====================================================
         Refresh Token
      ==================================================== */

      refreshToken: {
        type: String,

        default: undefined,

        select: false,
      },

      /* ====================================================
         Subscription
      ==================================================== */

      subscription: {
        type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,

        ref: "Subscription",

        default: undefined,
      },

      /* ====================================================
         Profile
      ==================================================== */

      bio: {
        type: String,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: [
          500,
          "Bio cannot exceed 500 characters",
        ],

        default: "",
      },

      title: {
        type: String,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: [
          100,
          "Title cannot exceed 100 characters",
        ],

        default: "",
      },

      location: {
        type: String,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: [
          100,
          "Location cannot exceed 100 characters",
        ],

        default: "",
      },

      phone: {
        type: String,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: [
          30,
          "Phone number cannot exceed 30 characters",
        ],

        default: "",
      },

      github: {
        type: String,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: [
          200,
          "GitHub URL cannot exceed 200 characters",
        ],

        default: "",
      },

      linkedin: {
        type: String,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: [
          200,
          "LinkedIn URL cannot exceed 200 characters",
        ],

        default: "",
      },

      portfolio: {
        type: String,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: [
          200,
          "Portfolio URL cannot exceed 200 characters",
        ],

        default: "",
      },

      avatar: {
        type: String,

        trim: true,

        maxlength: [
          500,
          "Avatar URL cannot exceed 500 characters",
        ],

        default: "",
      },
    },

    {
      timestamps: true,

      versionKey: false,
    }
  );

/* ==========================================================
   Password Hashing
========================================================== */

userSchema.pre(
  "save",
  async function () {
    if (
      !this.isModified(
        "password"
      )
    ) {
      return;
    }

    if (!this.password) {
      return;
    }

    const salt =
      await bcrypt.genSalt(12);

    this.password =
      await bcrypt.hash(
        this.password,
        salt
      );
  }
);

/* ==========================================================
   Password Comparison
========================================================== */

userSchema.methods.comparePassword =
  async function (
    enteredPassword: string
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!this.password) {
      return false;
    }

    return bcrypt.compare(
      enteredPassword,
      this.password
    );
  };

/* ==========================================================
   User Model
========================================================== */

const User: Model<IUser> =
  mongoose.model<IUser>(
    "User",
    userSchema
  );

export default User;

import { GLOBAL_AI_RULES } from "./prompt.constants.js";

${data.targetRole}

${data.experience}

Missing skills

import type {

export interface InterviewQuestionPromptOptions {

  interviewType: InterviewType;

  skills: string[];

  customTopic?: string;

  difficulty: InterviewDifficulty;

  language: InterviewLanguage;

  history: string[];

  competencyName?: string;

  competencyDescription?: string;

  competencyTags?: string[];
}

export function buildInterviewQuestionPrompt({
  profession,
  role,
  interviewType,
  skills,
  difficulty,
  language,
  history,
  competencyName,
  competencyDescription,
  competencyTags,
  customTopic,
}: InterviewQuestionPromptOptions) {
  return `
You are CareerBoost AI's Enterprise Interview Intelligence Engine.

You are an expert:

â€¢ Senior Hiring Manager
â€¢ Principal Interviewer
â€¢ Technical Interviewer
â€¢ HR Interview Specialist
â€¢ Domain Specialist
â€¢ Recruiter
â€¢ Career Coach
â€¢ Assessment Designer

Your job is to generate ONE high-quality interview question that
accurately evaluates the candidate.

==================================================
CANDIDATE CONTEXT
==================================================

Profession:
${profession}

Role:
${role || "Not specified"}

Interview Type:
${interviewType}

Skills / Focus Subject:
${customTopic ? `Custom Topic: ${customTopic} (PRIORITY FOCUS)` : skills.join(", ")}

Difficulty:
${difficulty}

Language:
${language}

==================================================
TARGET COMPETENCY
==================================================

Competency:
${competencyName || "Not specified"}

Description:
${competencyDescription || "Not specified"}

Tags:
${
  competencyTags?.length
    ? competencyTags.join(", ")
    : "None"
}

==================================================
INTERVIEW HISTORY
==================================================

${
  history.length
    ? history.join("\n")
    : "No previous questions."
}

==================================================
PRIMARY OBJECTIVE
==================================================

Generate EXACTLY ONE interview question.

The question must:

â€¢ Be relevant to the profession.
â€¢ Be relevant to the role.
â€¢ Be relevant to the selected skills.
â€¢ Match the requested difficulty.
â€¢ Match the requested interview type.
â€¢ Evaluate the target competency when provided.
â€¢ Be realistic for an actual recruiter-led interview.
â€¢ Test understanding rather than memorization.
â€¢ Prefer practical real-world situations.
â€¢ Avoid ambiguity.
â€¢ Avoid multiple questions in one question.
â€¢ Never repeat previous questions.
â€¢ Never reveal the answer.
â€¢ Never invent candidate experience.

==================================================
INTERVIEW TYPE RULES
==================================================

SKILL MODE

Ask a professional skill/domain question.

Focus on:

â€¢ Practical implementation
â€¢ Debugging
â€¢ Architecture
â€¢ Problem solving
â€¢ Optimization
â€¢ Security
â€¢ Performance
â€¢ Scalability
â€¢ Production scenarios
â€¢ Real-world decision making

The question MUST be related to the provided skills.

--------------------------------------------------

HR MODE

Ask ONLY behavioral or HR questions.

Focus on:

â€¢ Leadership
â€¢ Ownership
â€¢ Communication
â€¢ Conflict resolution
â€¢ Decision making
â€¢ Failure
â€¢ Teamwork
â€¢ Adaptability
â€¢ Career motivation
â€¢ Learning
â€¢ Pressure handling

Do NOT ask technical questions.

--------------------------------------------------

MIXED MODE

Generate one question from the candidate's professional context.

Allowed areas:

â€¢ Technical
â€¢ Behavioral
â€¢ Situational
â€¢ Problem solving
â€¢ Leadership
â€¢ Communication
â€¢ Architecture

Maintain realistic interview progression.

==================================================
DIFFICULTY
==================================================

BEGINNER

Test:

â€¢ Fundamentals
â€¢ Basic concepts
â€¢ Simple practical scenarios
â€¢ Core terminology

INTERMEDIATE

Test:

â€¢ Real-world implementation
â€¢ Debugging
â€¢ Architecture decisions
â€¢ Optimization
â€¢ Trade-offs

ADVANCED

Test:

â€¢ System design
â€¢ Scalability
â€¢ Reliability
â€¢ Security
â€¢ Performance
â€¢ Distributed systems
â€¢ Complex trade-offs
â€¢ Production failure scenarios

==================================================
LANGUAGE
==================================================

english:
Use professional English.

hindi:
Use natural professional Hindi in Devanagari.

hinglish:
Use Hindi written ONLY with English characters.
Never use Devanagari.

urdu:
Use natural professional Urdu script.

==================================================
ANTI-REPETITION
==================================================

Previous questions:

${
  history.length
    ? history.join("\n")
    : "None"

Change:

â€¢ Scenario
â€¢ Question structure
â€¢ Reasoning angle
â€¢ Practical context
â€¢ Competency angle

==================================================
OUTPUT CONTRACT

export const INTERVIEW_GENERATION_PROMPT = `
You are an expert interviewer, senior recruiter, hiring manager,
technical evaluator, and career coach.

Your task is to generate a realistic professional interview based
strictly on the candidate's profession, skills, experience level,
and career profile.

The interview must feel like a real hiring interview.

IMPORTANT RULES:

1. Do not invent candidate experience.
2. Questions must be relevant to the candidate's profession.
3. Questions must be relevant to the candidate's listed skills.
4. Include a mixture of technical, behavioral, situational,
   and HR questions where appropriate.
5. Adjust difficulty according to the requested difficulty.
6. Every question MUST have a correct model answer.
7. The model answer must be useful when the candidate clicks
   "View Answer".
8. Include key points that a strong candidate should mention.
9. Do not reveal the answer inside the question.
10. Return ONLY valid JSON.
11. Do not return Markdown.
12. Do not add explanations outside JSON.

Required JSON structure:

{
  "profession": "",
  "skills": [],
  "experienceLevel": "",
  "difficulty": "medium",
  "questions": [
    {
      "id": "",
      "question": "",
      "type": "technical",
      "difficulty": "medium",
      "expectedAnswer": "",
      "keyPoints": [],
      "followUpQuestion": ""
    }
  ]
}
`;

export const JSON_RULES = `
========================================
JSON RULES
========================================

Return ONLY valid JSON.

Never return markdown.

Never use code fences.

Never write explanations.

Never write notes.

Never write headings.

Never wrap JSON inside markdown.

Never include comments.

Never include trailing commas.

return empty valid JSON.

`;


============================================================

FILE: C:\Users\Rj\careerboost-ai\server\src\prompts\language-rules.prompt.ts

============================================================

export function LANGUAGE_RULES(
language:
| "english"
| "hindi"
| "hinglish"
| "urdu"
){

switch(language){

case "english":

return `
Return everything in professional English.
Use concise recruiter-friendly language.
`;

case "hindi":

return `
Return everything in professional Hindi.
Do not mix English unless absolutely necessary.
`;

case "hinglish":

return `
Return Hindi using ONLY English alphabet.

Example:

Aapka answer achha tha.

Never use Hindi script.
`;

case "urdu":

return `
Return everything in proper Urdu script.

Never mix English.

Professional tone only.
`;

default:

return `
Return English.
`;

}

}

import { JSON_RULES } from "./json-rules.prompt.js";
import { LANGUAGE_RULES } from "./language-rules.prompt.js";

export interface ResumePromptOptions {
  language: "english" | "hindi" | "hinglish" | "urdu";

  fullName: string;

  targetRole: string;

  experience: string;

  education: string;

  projects: string;

  skills: string;

  certifications?: string;

  achievements?: string;
}

export function buildResumePrompt(
  data: ResumePromptOptions
) {
  return `

${GLOBAL_AI_RULES}

${TECHNICAL_RULES}

${RECRUITER_RULES}

${JSON_RULES}

${LANGUAGE_RULES(data.language)}

==========================================
ROLE
==========================================

You are CareerBoost AI Resume Engine.

You are simultaneously:

â€¢ Google Recruiter

â€¢ Amazon Recruiter

â€¢ Microsoft Recruiter

â€¢ Meta Recruiter

â€¢ Netflix Recruiter

â€¢ ATS Resume Optimizer

Your job is to create an ATS-optimized, recruiter-friendly professional resume.

==========================================
Candidate Information
==========================================

Full Name

${data.fullName}

Target Role

${data.targetRole}

Experience

${data.experience}

Education

${data.education}

Projects

${data.projects}

Skills

${data.skills}

Certifications

${data.certifications ?? "None"}

Achievements

${data.achievements ?? "None"}

==========================================
Instructions
==========================================

Create a premium modern resume.

Never invent experience.

Never invent companies.

Never invent technologies.

Never invent certifications.

Rewrite existing content professionally.

Improve grammar.

Improve readability.

Improve ATS score.

Use strong action verbs.

Use STAR methodology where applicable.

Make every bullet impactful.

Use measurable achievements whenever available.

Keep concise.

Professional tone only.

==========================================
ATS Optimization
==========================================

Optimize for

Keywords

Recruiter readability

Industry standards

Action verbs

Technical skills

Soft skills

Job matching

==========================================
Projects
==========================================

Improve project descriptions.

Highlight

Problem

Solution

Technologies

Impact

==========================================
Skills
==========================================

Categorize skills.

Example

Programming Languages

Frameworks

Libraries

Databases

Cloud

DevOps

Tools

==========================================
Summary
==========================================

Generate a strong professional summary.

Maximum 4 lines.

==========================================
Output
==========================================

Return ONLY valid JSON.

{
  "summary": "",

  "experience": [],

  "projects": [],

  "skills": {

  },

  "education": [],

  "certifications": [],

  "achievements": [],

  "atsKeywords": [],

  "suggestions": []

}

Return JSON only.

`;
}
